We present PanoImager, an SfM-free framework that combines feed-forward pose/depth priors, geometry-conditioned diffusion view completion, and depth-guided 3DGS optimization. Given only a few panoramic images, PanoImager decomposes them into local perspective views, synthesizes auxiliary observations to enrich sparse evidence, and stabilizes Gaussian optimization for improved cross-view consistency. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show improved stability under extreme sparsity, suggesting PanoImager as an offline/background component for map refinement when SfM/SLAM fails to initialize.

</details>


### [145] [Pseudo-Text-Conditioned 3D Grounding DINO for Organ Localization in Abdominal CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27084)
*Siqi Chen,Han Gong,Keyi Hou,Jingxuan Yang,Sheethal Bhat,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-3GDINO，一个轻量级3D检测器，通过将Grounding-DINO风格架构适配到腹部CT器官定位，使用冻结的伪文本类令牌替代真实文本编码器。在193个CT体素上训练，最佳多尺度模型从头训练达到0.5830整体top-1类级mAP（IoU 0.1-0.7），粗定位表现优异（IoU 0.1时AP达0.9649），但严格框对齐仍有限（IoU 0.7时AP仅0.1552）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠腹部CT器官定位可为下游创伤分析提供空间先验，现有方法在3D器官定位中缺乏轻量级、可泛化的伪文本条件检测方案。

Method: 采用Grounding-DINO风格查询架构，使用冻结伪文本类令牌替代真实文本编码器；结合Swin3D视觉骨干、双向特征增强、伪文本引导查询选择和跨模态解码器，预测肝脏、脾脏、左肾、右肾和肠道五个器官的归一化3D边界框。

Result: 最佳多尺度模型从头训练，整体top-1类级mAP为0.5830（IoU阈值0.1-0.7）；粗定位强（IoU 0.1时AP 0.9649），严格框对齐有限（IoU 0.7时AP 0.1552）；优于固定骨干（0.5570）和可训练分类预训练骨干（0.4657）变体。

Conclusion: CT-3GDINO可作为伪文本条件3D器官定位的开源基线，未来工作应关注定位感知预训练、更丰富的多模态条件以及面向损伤的检测。

Abstract: Reliable organ localization in abdominal CT can provide spatial priors for downstream trauma analysis. We propose CT-3GDINO, a lightweight 3D detector that adapts a Grounding-DINO-style query-based architecture to fixed organ localization using frozen pseudo-text class tokens instead of a real text encoder. The model combines a Swin3D visual backbone, bidirectional feature enhancement, pseudo-text-guided query selection, and a cross-modality decoder to predict normalized 3D boxes for liver, spleen, left kidney, right kidney, and bowel. We train and evaluate on 193 matched RSNA/RATIC CT volumes with segmentation-derived boxes. The best multi-scale model, trained from scratch, achieves 0.5830 overall top-1 class-wise mAP over 3D IoU thresholds from 0.1 to 0.7, outperforming fixed- and trainable-backbone classification-pretrained variants with 0.5570 and 0.4657 mAP. Performance is strong for coarse localization, with 0.9649 AP at IoU 0.1, but remains limited for strict box alignment, with 0.1552 AP at IoU 0.7. These results establish CT-3GDINO as an open-source baseline for pseudo-text-conditioned 3D organ localization and motivate future work on localization-aware pretraining, richer multimodal conditioning, and injury-focused detection.

</details>


### [146] [SubdivAR: Autoregressive Next-Scale Prediction for Neural Mesh Subdivision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27088)
*Huipeng Guo,Zikai Song,Hang Long,Jielei Zhang,Wenbing Li,Junkai Lin,Tianhao Zhao,Jinshen Zhang,Tianle Guo,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SubdivAR，一种基于网格自回归表示（MAR）的神经网格细分框架，将细分重构为自回归的下一尺度预测任务，并引入混合拓扑感知Transformer以实现全局语义注意力和拓扑约束的局部特征聚合，在FII-40K数据集上训练，在Hausdorff距离和Chamfer距离上分别降低18.8%和14.2%，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的细分方案依赖固定局部细化规则，容易产生过度平滑的表面；现有神经细分方法虽改进了细节合成，但仍受限于局部建模且泛化能力有限。因此需要一种能够更好地恢复几何细节且具有更强泛化性的网格细分方法。

Method: 提出网格自回归表示（MAR），将不同细分层级的网格组织为有序尺度序列，将细分任务重构为自回归的下一尺度坐标预测。设计混合拓扑感知Transformer，融合全局语义注意力与拓扑约束的局部特征聚合，在每个细化阶段回归顶点偏移量以保留细分拓扑结构并恢复细粒度几何细节。构建了包含近40,000个高质量网格的FII-40K数据集用于训练。

Result: SubdivAR在Hausdorff距离上降低18.8%，在Chamfer距离上降低14.2%，显著优于现有最先进基线方法，并在复杂开放曲面几何上表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将网格细分建模为自回归下一尺度预测是一种有效范式，混合拓扑感知Transformer能够更好地融合全局语义与局部拓扑信息，SubdivAR在几何细节恢复和泛化能力上均优于传统规则方法和现有神经细分方法。

Abstract: Mesh subdivision is a fundamental operation for converting coarse, editable meshes into high-resolution surfaces, with broad applications in digital asset creation. Classical rule-based schemes rely on fixed local refinement rules and often produce over-smoothed surfaces. Recent neural subdivision methods improve detail synthesis, but remain constrained by local modeling and exhibit limited generalizability. We present SubdivAR, a neural mesh subdivision framework based on our proposed Mesh Autoregressive Representation (MAR). MAR arranges meshes at different subdivision levels into an ordered scale sequence, reformulating subdivision as autoregressive next-scale prediction. To support this formulation, we introduce a Hybrid Topology-Aware Transformer that combines global semantic attention with topology-constrained local feature aggregation. SubdivAR adopts a next-scale coordinate prediction paradigm, regressing vertex offsets at each refinement stage to preserve subdivision topology while recovering fine-grained geometric details. To enable reliable learning, we construct FII-40K, a curated dataset of nearly 40,000 high-quality meshes with multi-level subdivision supervision. Experiments show that SubdivAR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing Hausdorff Distance and Chamfer Distance by 18.8% and 14.2%, respectively, and demonstrates strong robustness on complex open-surface geometries.

</details>


### [147] [TMP: Tree-structured Mixed-policy Pruning for Large-scale Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27089)
*Peizhen Zhang,Yang Li,Xunsong Li,Songtao Liu,Zewen Liu,Qiangqiang Hu,Guotong Guo,Jupeng Ding,Yifu Sun,coopersli,Jian Zhang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TMP，首个树结构混合策略剪枝框架，可将HunyuanImage 3.0从80B压缩至20B（75%缩减），并将Z-Image turbo从6B压缩至4B（33%缩减），支持在单张24GB 4090 GPU上推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像生成模型规模快速增长，导致巨大的参数量、计算预算、资源需求和GPU显存占用，使得模型难以负担。

Method: 提出Tree-structured Mixed-policy Pruning（TMP）框架，可泛化应用于T2I和TI2I任务及MoE和DiT架构，并适用于步蒸馏模型作为最后阶段。

Result: HunyuanImage 3.0从80B压缩至20B（75%缩减），生成质量损失有限；Z-Image turbo从6B压缩至4B（33%缩减），质量退化可忽略；通过工程优化使20B版本可在单张24GB 4090 GPU上推理。

Conclusion: TMP框架能有效压缩大规模图像生成模型，大幅降低参数量和资源需求，同时保持可接受的生成质量，并已开源集成到现有仓库中。

Abstract: Modern image generation model rapidly grows their sizes to meet high-fidelity image synthesis. However, they gradually become unaffordable for their enormous parameter consumption and computation budget that lead to massive resources requirement and gpu memory footprint. In this paper, we propose TMP, the first Tree-structured Mixed-policy Pruning framework that generalizes prevalent image tasks (T2I and TI2I) and architectures (Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and Diffusion transformer (DiT)). It could be applied to the step-distilled models and contribute as the last stage. We perform experiments upon current open-sourced SOTA HunyuanImage-3.0 instruct and a popular efficient model Z-Image turbo. The proposed pruning framework manages to compress HunyuanImage 3.0 from 80B to 20B parameters at 75% reduction ratio, sacrificing limited generation quality. We also optimize to enable the inference of the pruned 20B version of HunyuanImage 3.0 on a single 24GB 4090 GPU by engineering skills. The inference script and model weight have been integrated into the existing HunyuanImage3.0 open-source github and huggingface repository. Besides, we prove the efficacy of TMP by compressing Z-Image turbo from 6B to 4B (33% reduction) with negligible degradation.

</details>


### [148] [DanceOPD: On-Policy Generative Field Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27377)
*Wei Zhou,Xiongwei Zhu,Zelin Xu,Bo Dong,Lixue Gong,Yongyuan Liang,Meng Chu,Leigang Qu,Lingdong Kong,Wei Liu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DanceOPD，一种基于on-policy生成场蒸馏的框架，用于流匹配模型，通过将每个能力源定义为速度场并在共享流状态空间上进行蒸馏，实现T2I、局部编辑和全局编辑能力的统一组合，同时避免能力间的相互干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像生成需要一个统一模型同时具备文本到图像（T2I）、局部编辑和全局编辑等多种能力，但这些能力天然不对齐且相互冲突（如编辑会降低T2I性能，全局与局部编辑相互干扰），如何有效组合这些能力成为核心挑战。

Method: 提出DanceOPD框架：采用on-policy生成场蒸馏，将每个能力源定义为流匹配模型共享流状态空间上的速度场；训练时将每个样本路由到一个能力场，查询低噪声的学生诱导状态，并采用简单的速度MSE损失进行训练；学生模型在其自身rollout状态上查询专家场，从而组合专家能力；该公式还可吸收算子定义的场（如无分类器引导）。

Result: 在T2I、编辑、真实感场吸收和CFG吸收任务上的综合实验表明，该方法改善了多能力组合，增强了目标能力同时保留了锚点生成质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为流匹配模型中的生成场蒸馏建立了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Modern image generation demands a single model that unifies diverse capabilities, including text-to-image (T2I), local editing, and global editing. However, these capabilities are rarely naturally aligned and often conflict. For instance, editing tends to degrade T2I performance, while global and local editing interfere with each other. Consequently, effectively composing these capabilities has become a central challenge for image generation model training. To tackle this, we introduce DanceOPD, an on-policy generative field distillation framework for flow-matching models that routes each sample to one capability field, queries one low-noise student-induced state, and trains with a simple velocity MSE objective. With each capability source defined as a velocity field over the shared flow state space, the student learns from fields queried on its own rollout states to compose expert capabilities. This formulation also absorbs operator-defined fields such as classifier-free guidance. Comprehensive experiments on T2I, editing, realism-field absorption, and CFG absorption show that our approach improves multi-capability composition, strengthening target capabilities while preserving anchor generation quality. We believe this work establishes a practical route for generative field distillation in flow-matching models.

</details>


### [149] [FlameVQA: A Physically-Grounded UAV Wildfire VQA Benchmark with Radiometric Thermal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27128)
*Mobin Habibpour,John Spodnik,Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlameVQA——一个针对无人机野火监测的多选题视觉问答基准数据集，基于FLAME 3数据集构建，结合RGB图像和辐射热红外TIFF数据实现温度验证的推理。包含每张图像34道多选题，覆盖6个操作能力组，并通过MLLM辅助标注、确定性热规则和人工审核确保标签质量。评估显示当前MLLM在有明确跨模态线索时表现良好，但在浓烟下的检测和覆盖范围估计任务中显著失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-only视觉方法在复杂空中场景（烟雾、尺度变化、遮挡）下对野火监测的推理能力不足，缺乏结合热红外模态的温度可验证、安全关键的问答基准。

Method: 基于FLAME 3数据集，为每张图像设计34道多选题（分属6个操作能力组：检测、定位、分布/覆盖估计、跨模态推理、飞行规划等）；采用MLLM辅助标注+确定性热红外规则+跨问题一致性检查+人工审核的混合标注流程。

Result: 代表性MLLM在提供明确跨模态线索时表现强劲，但在浓烟场景下的存在性检测和覆盖范围估计任务上出现显著失败。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM需要领域特定的适配（domain-specific adaptation）才能更好地支持灾害与野火监测任务。

Abstract: Wildfire monitoring from UAVs requires reliable reasoning over complex aerial scenes, where smoke, scale variation, and occlusions often limit RGB-only interpretation. We introduce FlameVQA, a multiple-choice visual question answering benchmark for UAV-based wildfire intelligence built on FLAME 3, leveraging paired RGB imagery and radiometric thermal TIFFs for temperature-grounded, safety-critical reasoning. FlameVQA includes 34 multiple-choice questions per image spanning six operational capability groups, covering tasks such as detection, localization, distribution/coverage estimation, cross-modal reasoning, and flight planning. To ensure label reliability, we combine MLLM-assisted annotation with deterministic thermal rules and cross-question consistency checks, followed by human auditing. We also evaluate representative MLLMs on FlameVQA to provide baselines for future work. Results show strong performance when explicit cross-modal cues are available, but notable failures on presence detection under heavy smoke and on coverage estimation. These findings suggest that current MLLMs require domain-specific adaptation to better support disaster and wildfire monitoring. The dataset and benchmark code are open-source at github.com/mobiiin/WildFire_VQA

</details>


### [150] [Safe Autoregressive Image Generation with Iterative Self-Improving Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27147)
*Yunqi Xue,Zhijiang Li,Philip Torr,Jindong Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代式自改进码本方法，用于提升自回归统一多模态模型在文本到图像生成任务中的安全性。该方法利用模型自身的理解和判断能力识别不安全图像，通过构建有害空间并更新码本来消除有害映射，再在无害空间中对码本进行自适应微调以保证图像质量，整个过程无需人工标注和外部反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归统一多模态模型在文本到图像生成中展现出潜力，但其安全性尚未被充分研究。这些模型通过离散化视觉令牌生成图像，码本中的有害映射可能导致生成不安全内容。现有方法依赖外部标注或反馈，成本高昂且不可扩展。

Method: 两步迭代流程：(1) 利用模型自身识别不安全生成结果，构建有害图像-文本对，定义"有害空间"并据此更新码本以消除有害映射；(2) 在无害空间中使用安全图像-文本对进行码本自适应微调，保持生成质量。两步重复直至收敛。

Result: 在无需外部反馈和人工标注的条件下，模型的安全性得到迭代式提升，同时生成质量得以保持。

Conclusion: 自回归统一多模态模型可以利用自身能力实现自我安全改进，码本修正是一种有效的安全增强途径，无需依赖外部监督。

Abstract: Unlike diffusion-based models that operate in continuous latent spaces, autoregressive unified multimodal models produce images by sequentially predicting discretized visual tokens. These tokens are derived from a codebook that maps embeddings to quantized visual patterns. The language-like architecture enables unified multimodal models to effectively capture text conditional information for generation, making them promising for text-to-image tasks. This also raises an interesting question: how safe are the images generated in such an autoregressive way? In this work, we propose iterative self-improving codebooks for safe autoregressive generation. We leverage the understanding and judgment capabilities of the unified multimodal model itself to identify unsafe generated images without human annotation. Subsequently, the inherent representations in the codebook are fixed to eliminate harmful mappings. Our method comprises two steps: first, we use the unified model to identify unsafe generations and construct corresponding harmful and safe image-text pairs. These pairs are used to construct the Harmful Space and guide updates to the codebook, thereby eliminating harmful outputs. Second, we perform adaptive fine-tuning on the codebook within the harmless space using safe image-text pairs to ensure the quality of generated images. These two steps are repeated until no further improvement is observed, producing a safety-enhanced model codebook. Without additional external feedback, the safety of models is improved iteratively.

</details>


### [151] [HarmVideoBench: Benchmarking Harmful Video Understanding in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27187)
*Jiajun Wu,Haoyu Kang,Yining Sun,Jiacheng Hou,Heng Zhang,Danyang Zhang,Zhenjun Zhao,Haochi Zhang,Leixin Sun,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Yushan Li,Ruiyu Li,Mengkai Huang,Yan Gao,Xu Zhang,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HarmVideoBench是一个多层次有害视频理解诊断基准，包含1,379个视频和4,137道多选题，从三个维度评估LVLM对有害视频的理解能力，并提出了BCR方法将宏平均性能从61.7%提升至84.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害视频基准存在两个问题：1) 忽略有害视频的多层特征，仅作为二分类任务评估，无法捕捉隐性或深层上下文危害；2) 缺乏解释性理由，模型可能通过表面捷径成功而无法解释原因。

Method: 构建包含1,379个视频和4,137道多选题的HarmVideoBench基准，定义三个层次维度：可观察证据、剪辑内部含义和跨剪辑推理；提出BCR方法，预测推理边界并仅在需要时动态检索上下文。

Result: 在19个领先模型上评估，BCR方法将基线的宏平均性能从61.7%提升至84.4%，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: HarmVideoBench能够有效评估LVLM对有害视频的深层理解能力，BCR方法显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently shown immense potential in automated content moderation, sparking growing interest in developing harmful-video benchmarks. However, we identify two primary limitations in existing works: 1) The multi-layered characteristics of harmful videos are overlooked. Existing benchmarks predominantly formulate evaluation as a binary classification task, failing to capture implicit or deep contextual harms. 2) Explanatory rationales are completely absent. Current frameworks measure exclusively whether a model flags a video correctly rather than explaining why, turning evaluation into a black box where models can succeed through superficial shortcuts. To address these problems, we present HarmVideoBench, a multi-layered diagnostic benchmark comprising 1,379 videos paired with 4,137 multiple-choice questions. HarmVideoBench benchmarks three hierarchical dimensions: Observable Evidence, Clip-Internal Meaning, and Beyond-Clip Reasoning, aiming to evaluate models' deep understanding beyond surface cues with carefully balanced and curated samples. We evaluate 19 leading models on HarmVideoBench to assess their multidimensional understanding of harmful videos. Moreover, we introduce BCR, a benchmark-aligned method that predicts reasoning boundaries and dynamically retrieves context only when needed. Experimental results show that BCR substantially improves the base model's performance in harmful video understanding, raising the macro average from 61.7 percent to a state-of-the-art 84.4 percent.

</details>


### [152] [LISA: Likelihood Score Alignment for Visual-condition Controllable Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27192)
*Yanghao Wang,Hongxu Chen,Jiazhen Liu,Zhenqi He,Rui Liu,Zhen Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA方法，通过将侧网络中间特征与近似似然分数对齐，作为正则化手段，加速了视觉条件可控生成中双分支范式的训练收敛并提升生成质量，且几乎不增加训练成本、零额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流双分支范式（侧网络编码视觉条件、冻结主网络）虽广泛应用，但侧分支的作用及其训练效率尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 从得分生成模型视角重新解释双分支范式：主网络提供先验无条件分数，侧网络隐式贡献似然分数。2) 提出似然分数对齐（LISA）正则化方法：从侧网络指定层hook特征，通过轻量解码器投影到分数潜空间；构造近似似然分数目标，计算解码器输出与目标的距离作为额外正则化损失；联合优化侧网络和解码器，使用标准扩散损失和正则化损失。

Result: 在多种图像/视频任务、架构和扩散/流模型上，LISA一致地加速训练收敛、提升最终合成结果，同时使侧网络特征更解耦地用于条件建模，且几乎不增加训练成本、零额外推理成本。

Conclusion: LISA是一种有效的正则化方法，能提升双分支可控生成范式的训练效率和生成质量，且开销极小。

Abstract: The prevalent dual-branch paradigm, i.e., training a side network to encode visual conditions and fusing its intermediate-layer features to a frozen pretrained main network, has shown remarkable success in visual-condition controllable generation. Despite its widespread adoption, the role of the side branch and its training efficiency remain underexplored. In this paper, we first revisit this mainstream paradigm through the lens of score-based generative modeling: 1) The main network preserves visual perceptual quality by providing a prior unconditional score. 2) The side network steers conditional control by implicitly contributing a likelihood score. Guided by this perspective, we propose LIkelihood Score Alignment (LISA), an effective regularization method that explicitly aligns the intermediate feature of the side network with an approximated likelihood score. Specifically, we first hook features from a designated layer of the side network and project them into the score latent space by a lightweight decoder. Then, we construct an approximated likelihood score target and calculate the distance between the decoder's output and this target as an additional regularization loss. Finally, we jointly optimize the side network and decoder with both standard diffusion loss and our regularization loss. Experiments across various image/video tasks, architectures, and diffusion/flow models demonstrated that LISA can not only consistently accelerate the training convergence and improve final synthetic results, but also encourage the side network's features to be more disentangled for conditional modeling with negligible additional training cost and zero extra inference cost.

</details>


### [153] [SatSplatDiff: Geometry-preserving generative refinement for high-fidelity satellite Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27223)
*Jiyong Kim,Shuang Song,Ronjgun Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatSplatDiff是一种针对卫星3D重建的生成式细化方法，通过单目深度监督、多尺度几何细化和阴影引导的生成式细化，在提升视觉质量的同时减少几何退化，在IARPA2016和DFC2019数据集上实现了SOTA性能，几何MAE降低18%，视觉保真度提升28-45%，支持5倍分辨率增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅在卫星3D重建中存在建筑立面监督不足导致表面空洞和视觉质量下降的问题；而生成式细化方法虽能提升视觉保真度，但逐视图独立细化会破坏光度一致性，引入幻觉并导致几何退化。作者旨在最小化生成式细化中普遍存在的几何退化问题。

Method: 1) 基于SatSplat的DSM初始化和2DGS阴影投射；2) 引入单目深度监督和多尺度几何细化，建立几何精确且规则化的表面表示；3) 应用阴影引导的生成式细化，利用几何计算的阴影图引导高斯体保持与底层几何的一致性，在提升视觉保真度的同时减少几何退化。

Result: 在IARPA2016和DFC2019数据集上，几何MAE降低高达18%，视觉保真度（FID-CLIP）提升28-45%，支持高达5倍分辨率增强且幻觉极少，传感器一致性良好，跨瓦片一致性和大规模重建可扩展性强。

Conclusion: SatSplatDiff通过阴影引导的生成式细化有效平衡了视觉质量提升与几何保真度保持之间的矛盾，在卫星3D重建任务中取得了最优性能，并具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has been recently explored for satellite 3D reconstruction, demonstrating flexibility and efficiency in representing radiometrically diverse satellite scenes. However, the limited top viewpoint of satellite imagery results in insufficient supervision on building facades, leaving surface holes and degraded visual fidelity. Generative refinement, which leverages pretrained generative priors to iteratively refine and update the rendered images used as supervision targets, has recently been investigated to improve the visual fidelity of Gaussian-rendered images. However, since these models refine each view independently, the resulting images can generate hallucinations and break photo-consistency, leading to geometric degradation. To address these limitations, we propose SatSplatDiff, which aims to minimize geometric degradation prevalent in generative refinement. Building on photogrammetric DSM initialization and 2DGS-based shadow casting established in our prior work SatSplat, we first introduce monocular depth supervision and multi-scale geometric refinement to establish a geometrically accurate and well-regularized surface representation. We then apply shadow-guided generative refinement, where geometrically calculated shadow maps guide the Gaussians to maintain consistency with the underlying geometry, improving visual fidelity while reducing geometric degradation. Extensive evaluations on the IARPA2016 and DFC2019 datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing geometric MAE by up to 18% and improving visual fidelity (FID-CLIP) by 28-45% over existing baselines. Our method delivers up to 5x resolution enhancement with minimal hallucination and sensor-consistent appearance, demonstrating seamless cross-tile consistency and strong scalability for large-scale reconstruction. Source code is available at https://github.com/GDAOSU/SatSplatDiff

</details>


### [154] [CORTEX: A Structured Reasoning Benchmark for Trustworthy 3D Chest CT MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27264)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Anees Ur Rehman Hashmi,Numan Saeed,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan,Christoph Lippert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CORTEX是一个面向3D胸部CT的结构化推理基准，通过四阶段诊断追踪（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成）恢复专家报告中缺失的推理过程，包含76,177条经自动化评分和放射科专家验证的推理轨迹，为可解释、可验证的多模态大模型推理提供了结构化监督和分阶段评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在医学影像中的推理难以解释和验证，尤其是3D放射学中诊断应可追溯到扫描证据；现有的胸部CT问答数据集将专家报告简化为仅答案对，丢弃了连接发现与结论的推理过程，并省略了临床依赖的病史信息，导致缺乏训练和验证推理能力所需的结构化监督和评估协议。

Method: 与临床医生紧密合作设计四阶段诊断推理结构（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成）；利用前沿大语言模型生成推理轨迹，结合自动化评分和放射科专家审查进行分阶段筛选和验证；基于CT-RATE数据集构建。

Result: 构建了包含76,177条经过验证的推理轨迹的数据集，覆盖开放式VQA、封闭式VQA和报告生成三类任务，同时提供了分阶段评估协议。

Conclusion: CORTEX为3D胸部CT的可信推理模型提供了结构化监督和分阶段评估协议，填补了该领域推理可解释性和可验证性的空白。

Abstract: Reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, this reasoning is usually free-form text judged only by its final answer, making it hard to interpret and verify, especially in 3D radiology, where a diagnosis should be traceable to evidence in the scan. Existing chest CT question-answering datasets compound this by reducing expert radiology reports to answer-only pairs, dropping the reasoning that links findings to conclusions and omitting the patient history clinicians rely on. As a result, reasoning-capable 3D chest CT MLLMs remain out of reach, as neither the structured supervision needed to train them nor the protocol needed to verify their reasoning yet exists. We introduce CORTEX (Clinically Organized Reasoning and sTructured EXplanation), a structured reasoning benchmark for 3D chest CT. For each question, CORTEX restores the missing reasoning as a four-stage diagnostic trace mirroring a radiologist's workflow: task understanding, visual observation, diagnostic reasoning, and answer synthesis. We generate these traces using frontier large language models with broad medical and general-domain knowledge, then filter and verify them with a stage-level evaluation protocol combining automated rubric scoring with expert radiologist review. Crucially, both the reasoning structure and evaluation rubrics are designed in close collaboration with clinicians. Built on CT-RATE, a large, publicly available chest CT dataset without reasoning annotations, CORTEX comprises 76,177 validated reasoning traces across open-ended VQA, closed-ended VQA, and report generation, providing both the structured supervision and the stage-level evaluation protocol needed to build and evaluate trustworthy reasoning models for 3D chest CT. Our dataset and evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [155] [Exact and Deterministic Patch Descriptor Retrieval via Hierarchical Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27280)
*Koichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层归一化（HN）方案，通过将特征向量拆分为主/次分量，利用柯西-施瓦茨不等式推导相似度上界，实现确定性的精确最近邻搜索，在仅评估0.4%条目的情况下达到13.7倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻（ANN）方法（如HNSW、IVF-PQ）为追求速度牺牲了精确度和确定性，不同运行可能返回不同结果。本文旨在设计一种既保持精确（等同于穷举全向量搜索）又具有确定性（相同输入始终输出相同结果）的高效补丁描述符检索方法。

Method: 提出分层归一化（HN）方案：将预归一化特征向量拆分为K维主分量（范数sqrt(1-alpha)）和(128-K)维次分量（范数sqrt(alpha)）。由于次分量内积被alpha界约束（柯西-施瓦茨不等式），主分量相似度加alpha构成全相似度的可容许上界。搜索时先扫描所有条目的K维主分量，仅对无法剪枝的条目进行完整的128维评估——这是一种可证明精确的分支定界扫描。训练采用HN修改的HardNet，在UBC补丁数据集的notredame分割上训练，在trevi和halfdome上评估。采用缓存优化的结构数组（SoA）布局。

Result: 在trevi上实现13.7倍加速，halfdome上12.7倍加速，仅0.4%条目需要完整评估。K=16, alpha=1/8时，trevi上FPR@95从0.0062微升至0.0064，加速7.2倍，98.8%条目跳过完整评估。

Conclusion: HN方案能够在保持与穷举搜索完全相同精确度的前提下实现显著加速，且具有完全确定性（不受运行顺序、线程数或硬件影响），为需要可复现精确结果的检索场景提供了替代ANN的有效方案。

Abstract: We present a patch descriptor retrieval method that returns the exact nearest neighbour -- provably identical to exhaustive full-vector search -- while evaluating only a small fraction of the database, and does so deterministically: the same (database, query) pair always produces the same result, independent of run order, thread count, or hardware. This contrasts with approximate nearest-neighbour (ANN) approaches such as HNSW and IVF-PQ, which trade exactness for speed and may return different results across runs. The enabling mechanism is Hierarchical Normalization (HN): a normalisation scheme that splits the pre-normalisation feature vector into a K-dim major component (norm sqrt(1-alpha)) and a (128-K)-dim minor component (norm sqrt(alpha)). Since the minor inner product is bounded by alpha (Cauchy-Schwarz on the prescribed norms), the major similarity plus alpha is an admissible upper bound on the full similarity: the search scans the K-dim major component for all entries, then applies full 128-dim evaluation only to entries that cannot be pruned -- a provably exact branch-and-bound scan. We train HN-modified HardNet on the notredame split of the UBC patch dataset and evaluate on trevi and halfdome. With a cache-optimised Structure-of-Arrays layout and K=8, alpha=1/32, the search achieves 13.7x (trevi) / 12.7x (halfdome) speed-up over brute-force 128-dim search, with only 0.4% of entries requiring full evaluation. At K=16, alpha=1/8, FPR@95 rises from 0.0062 to 0.0064 on trevi at 7.2x speed-up, with 98.8% of entries bypassing full evaluation.

</details>


### [156] [Sculpting NeRF Geometry: Human-Preference Fine-Tuning of a 3D-Aware Face GAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27305)
*Archer Moore,Mingming Gong,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需网格或形状先验的3D生成式RLHF方法，直接在辐射场密度(σ)上通过奖励模型微调EG3D，仅使用少量偏好样本即可改善面部几何，用户偏好率达74.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF用于3D生成的方法大多需要优化显式表面表示（如将辐射场转换为网格），依赖表面监督数据。本文旨在直接对辐射场密度进行奖励学习，避免外部网格或形状先验，实现更简洁的几何优化。

Method: 基于预训练的3D感知生成模型EG3D，在NeRF的连续3D密度场(σ)上训练奖励模型，无需预训练，仅需少量偏好样本；采用密度一致性约束保持2D外观质量，仅提供几何学习信号，无需文本条件、网格提取或多视角渲染。

Result: 在面部的无条件3D生成任务中，FID-50k从4.09上升至6.66（外观质量略有下降但有界），用户偏好测试中74.4%的成对比较选择微调后的几何结果。

Conclusion: 证明了直接在辐射场密度上进行RLHF微调是有效的几何优化方式，无需外部形状先验或网格转换，且仅需单标注者的少量偏好样本即可获得用户偏好的几何改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for 3D generation is now established across a number of works, but most existing pipelines optimise explicit surface representations, often by converting radiance fields into meshes and training heavily on surface-supervised data. We instead fine-tune a pretrained 3D-aware generative model directly from a learned reward over radiance-field density ($σ$) values, with no externally supplied mesh or shape prior. The reward model requires no pretraining, trains easily on a small set of preference samples, and yields robust improvement in 3D geometry. Working on an unconditional 3D-aware face GAN (EG3D), our reward reads the continuous 3D density field of the neural radiance field (NeRF) directly and supplies a geometry-only learning signal, requiring neither text conditioning, mesh extraction, nor multi-view rendering. A density-consistency constraint keeps the 2D appearance qualitatively similar while the geometry is reshaped, at a measurable but bounded distributional cost (FID-50k rises from 4.09 to 6.66): the fine-tuned generator, trained from the preferences of a single annotator as a proof of concept, produces face geometries preferred by users in 74.4% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [157] [See & Sniff: Learning Visuo-Olfactory Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27307)
*Seongyu Kim,Seungwoo Lee,Hyeonggon Ryu,Joon Son Chung,Arda Senocak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SmellNet-V（大规模视觉-嗅觉数据集）和See & Sniff（自监督联合表征学习框架），通过语义对齐将单模态嗅觉数据转为跨模态基准，在气味分类、跨模态检索和气味定位任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多模态模型已整合视觉与语言、音频或触觉，但嗅觉因缺乏配对的视觉-嗅觉数据而几乎未被探索。解决这一数据瓶颈是推动视觉-嗅觉多模态学习的核心动机。

Method: 基于"气味身份在语义类别内对视觉变换具有不变性"的洞察，将仅有气味的样本与语义对齐的网络图像合成配对，构建SmellNet-V数据集；提出See & Sniff自监督框架，通过密集局部对齐学习联合视觉-嗅觉表征，并自然产生气味显著性图用于气味源的空间定位。

Result: 在纯气味分类任务上超越仅用气味的基线7%；在跨模态检索和气味定位任务上表现优异，建立了视觉-嗅觉学习作为多模态感知新方向的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了无需昂贵联合采集即可实现有效的视觉-嗅觉跨模态学习，为多模态感知开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While modern multimodal models integrate vision with language, audio, or touch, olfaction remains largely unexplored due to the lack of paired visuo-olfactory data. We introduce SmellNet-V, a scalable visuo-olfactory dataset built on the insight that odor identity is largely invariant to visual transformations within a semantic category. This allows us to synthetically pair smell-only samples with semantically aligned in-the-wild web images, converting a unimodal olfactory dataset into a cross-modal benchmark without costly co-collection. Building on this dataset, we propose See & Sniff, a self-supervised framework that learns joint visuo-olfactory representations via dense local alignment and naturally produces smell saliency maps for spatial grounding of odor sources. We further introduce pixel-level smell localization task and a benchmark for evaluation. Our method surpasses smell-only baselines by 7% in smell classification from smell alone and generalizes to cross-modal retrieval and smell localization, establishing visuo-olfactory learning as a new direction in multimodal perception.

</details>


### [158] [ViQ: Text-Aligned Visual Quantized Representations at Any Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27313)
*Xumin Yu,Zuyan Liu,Zhenyu Yang,Yuhao Dong,Shengsheng Qian,Jiwen Lu,Han Hu,Yongming Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViQ提出了一种统一的视觉量化表示框架，通过两阶段量化学习（文本对齐预训练+特征离散化）在离散表示中平衡语义与细节，支持原生分辨率输入，在多模态任务上取得与连续高维特征表示相竞争的性能，并实现20%-70%的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉离散表示方法难以平衡低级细节与高级语义：面向重建的表示缺乏语义信息，而语义强的特征又存在严重细节损失。同时，统一文本与视觉的离散表示有助于简化多模态建模并提高训练效率。

Method: 两阶段量化学习框架：(1) 文本对齐预训练——利用预训练语言模型增强视觉编码器的语义监督，支持原生分辨率输入；(2) 特征离散化——提出近端表示学习策略逐步压缩特征空间，以及位置感知的逐头量化机制以灵活处理任意分辨率。

Result: 在多模态任务上，ViQ取得与SOTA连续高维视觉特征表示相竞争的性能，同时保持低级重建的高精度；多模态训练效率提升20%-70%。

Conclusion: ViQ作为统一的通用离散视觉表示框架，成功在离散表示中兼顾语义与细节，支持原生分辨率，兼具高性能与高效率，为多模态建模提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: A unified representation for text and vision is a natural pursuit, as it enables simpler multimodal modeling and more efficient training. However, representing images as discrete signals in the same way as text inevitably introduces severe information loss. Existing work struggles to balance low-level details and high-level semantics in discrete representations: reconstruction-oriented representations often lack semantic information, whereas semantically stronger features typically suffer from severe loss of detail. We present ViQ, a Visual Quantized Representations framework, which is designed to balance semantics and details in discrete representations while supporting inputs at native resolutions, thereby enabling it to serve as a unified and general discrete representation for arbitrary visual inputs. Our approach structures quantization learning into two stages: text-aligned pre-training and feature discretization. With text-aligned pre-training, we enhance the visual encoder semantic-rich supervision from the pretrained language model and enable it to process native-resolution visual inputs. During discretization, we propose a proximal representation learning strategy to progressively compact the feature space, along with a position-aware head-wise quantization mechanism that enables flexible processing of arbitrary resolutions. Extensive experiments on multimodal tasks demonstrate that ViQ achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art multimodal vision encoders with continuous and high-dimensional visual features, while maintaining high precision in low-level reconstruction. We also show that multimodal training with visual quantized representations largely improves efficiency, yielding up to 20\%-70\% acceleration with different base LLMs and training recipes.

</details>


### [159] [OctoSense: Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27317)
*Anthony Bisulco,Jeremy Wang,Kostas Daniilidis,Randall Balestriero,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctoSense是一个开源传感器平台及数据集，包含59小时多传感器驾驶数据，并提出了一种"晚融合"掩码自编码器架构，在多模态自监督学习、鲁棒性和推理速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人感知系统缺乏覆盖多种传感器类型（包括退化情况）的大规模真实世界同步数据集，且缺乏能有效融合异构传感器（不同表示、频率、延迟和噪声）的自监督学习框架。

Method: 提出"晚融合"掩码自编码器架构：使用模态特定的分词器处理不同传感器的时空特征，在推理时缓存模态特定token以应对异步输入流，实现多模态自监督学习。

Result: 在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别仅需6.68ms和112ms计算表征；在光流、深度、语义分割和自运动估计等任务上优于纯图像基础模型；在夜间或传感器退化场景下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OctoSense提供了大规模的异构传感器平台和数据集，其提出的晚融合掩码自编码器能够高效、鲁棒地融合多模态数据，为机器人感知系统提供了新的基准。

Abstract: We present OctoSense, an open-source sensor platform with stereo RGB and event cameras, LiDAR, a thermal camera, an inertial measurement unit, RTK-corrected global positioning system, and proprioception (CAN bus data from a car, and joint angles for a quadruped robot). The eponymous OctoSense dataset contains 59 hours of time-synchronized driving data across different types of environments at different times of the day, including situations with highly degraded sensors. We demonstrate multi-modal self-supervised learning using such real-world robotics data, where sensors have different representations, frequencies, latencies and noise. Our approach, a "late-fusion" masked autoencoder, (i) uses modality-specific tokenizers to account for different spatiotemporal characteristics of these sensors, and (ii) caches modality-specific tokens at inference time to process new measurements as they come. This architecture (i) is fast (6.68 ms and 112 ms on NVIDIA 5090 and Orin NX respectively, to compute the representation), (ii) performs better than existing image-only foundation models on tasks such as estimation of optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion (translation, rotation, and steering angle), and (iii) predicts robustly at nighttime or in situations where sensory data is degraded. See our project page for links to the dataset, code, and supplementary videos: https://abisulco.com/octosense/.

</details>


### [160] [Not All Actions Are Equal: Rethinking Conditioning for Dexterous World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27325)
*Zizhao Yuan,Zhengtu Liang,Taowen Wang,Qiwei Liang,Yichi Wang,Yunheng Wang,Yuetong Fang,Lusong Li,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DexAC-WM，通过结构化动作条件建模（动作标记化+局部细化+全局调制）和语义分支（提供物体-场景先验）解决高自由度灵巧操作中的动作条件建模问题，在EgoDex和EgoVerse数据集上显著提升了FID、FVD和PCK指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型将整个动作序列压缩为单一表示，这在低自由度控制中有效，但在高自由度场景下不可靠。高自由度灵巧动作具有异构性（跨越多个数量级），统一聚合会导致优化不平衡，影响细粒度效果建模和动作保真度。

Method: DexAC-WM包含：1) 结构化动作条件：通过动作标记化保留维度级语义，结合局部细化与全局调制对齐动作信号与视觉动态；2) 语义分支：引入物体-场景先验，增强世界模型的高层语义基础，支持高自由度动作条件视频预测。

Result: 在EgoDex和EgoVerse数据集上，结合语义分支的DexAC在FID、FVD和PCK指标上显著提升，展示了视觉-时间真实性和动作跟随一致性的改进。DexAC可扩展到其他骨干网络，验证了结构化动作条件设计的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将世界模型扩展到高自由度控制需要结构化动作建模和语义基础两方面支撑。

Abstract: Recent advances in action-conditioned world models show promising progress in modeling complex interactions and forecasting future states under diverse action sequences. While these models are often driven by stronger visual representations and model capacity, action conditioning itself remains underexplored. Most existing approaches compress the entire action sequence into a single representation, which works well for low-DoF control but becomes less reliable in high-DoF scenarios. We observe that high-DoF dexterous actions are inherently heterogeneous, spanning multiple orders of magnitude, where large-scale motions coexist with subtle but important signals. When uniformly aggregated, optimization exhibits an imbalance across action components, which hinders the modeling of fine-grained effects and affects action fidelity. We therefore propose DexAC-WM, which treats action conditioning as a structured process rather than global compression. DexAC preserves dimension-level semantics via action tokenization and aligns action signals with visual dynamics through local refinement and global modulation. To address the limited high-level semantic grounding in existing world models, we further introduce a semantic branch that provides rich object-scene priors, which enables world model to capture dynamic visual details while supporting high-DoF action-conditioned video prediction. Experiments on EgoDex and EgoVerse show that combining the semantic branch with DexAC significantly improves FID, FVD, and PCK, demonstrating gains in visual-temporal realism and action-following consistency. We further verify that DexAC extends to other backbones, showing the scalability of our structured action-conditioning design. These results suggest that scaling world models to high-DoF control requires both structured action modeling and semantic grounding.

</details>


### [161] [RoPEMover: Depth-Aware Object Relocation via Positional Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27332)
*Ipek Oztas,Duygu Ceylan,Aybars Bugra Aksoy,Aysegul Dundar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散Transformer的位置编码操作实现单图像几何感知物体运动的方法，通过深度感知的RoPE编码在3D空间中进行物体位移，在保持场景一致性的同时实现最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在单图像物体运动任务中难以保持几何一致性，特别是在处理遮挡、生成先前未见过区域以及维持阴影和反射等场景级效果方面存在不足。

Method: 将2D RoPE扩展为深度感知的3D空间编码形式，直接操作扩散Transformer的位置表征来实现可控运动；使用合成数据结合少量真实图像通过参数高效微调进行训练。

Result: 在标准物体运动基准测试中，所有评估指标均达到最先进水平，能够在大位移下保持物体身份、生成合理的遮挡区域内容并一致地更新阴影和光照等场景依赖效果。

Conclusion: 通过操作位置编码而非修改网络架构即可实现几何一致的物体运动，且仅需少量真实数据即可获得优异的场景一致性表现。

Abstract: Moving an object in a single image requires geometry-consistent spatial rearrangement, including handling occlusions, revealing previously unseen regions, and maintaining coherent shadows and reflections. Existing approaches are not well suited to this setting and often fail to preserve such scene-level consistency. We address this problem by introducing a geometry-aware object motion method that operates directly on the positional representations of diffusion transformers. Our key insight is that rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) define a structured spatial field that can be explicitly manipulated to induce controlled motion. We extend 2D RoPE into a depth-aware formulation that encodes 3D spatial structure, enabling consistent object displacement and scene-aware updates. Our model is trained using synthetic data combined with a small set of real images via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Despite minimal real supervision, it preserves object identity under large spatial displacements, generates plausible content in newly revealed regions, and consistently updates scene-dependent effects such as shadows and illumination. Experimental results on standard object motion benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [162] [SAM2Matting: Generalized Image and Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27339)
*Ruiqi Shen,Guangquan Jie,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM2Matting 提出了一种跟踪器到抠图的框架，通过增强基础跟踪器（如SAM2/SAM3）并添加区域提议桥接和专用抠图头部，仅使用图像训练即可在视频抠图上达到SOTA性能，支持多种提示类型，保持强时间一致性，并在人物和野外场景中展示出鲁棒的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频抠图面临高层跟踪（需要帧级理解）与低层抠图（关注极精细细节）之间的固有鸿沟，现有方法依赖昂贵且范围有限的视频抠图数据集，限制了域外泛化并损害了跟踪鲁棒性。

Method: 将视频抠图任务解耦为两个组件：1) 基础跟踪器（如SAM2、SAM3）负责时间一致性跟踪；2) 区域提议桥接和专用抠图头部负责精细细节解析。仅使用图像训练，无需视频抠图数据。

Result: 仅通过图像训练，在视频抠图任务上达到新的SOTA性能；支持多种提示类型；保持强时间一致性；在人物中心和自然场景中均表现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAM2Matting 通过解耦跟踪与抠图，证明了无需视频级训练数据即可实现高质量视频抠图，为视频抠图领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Despite impressive advances in image matting, video matting remains challenging due to the inherent gap between high-level tracking, which requires frame-wise understanding, and low-level matting, which focuses on extremely fine-grained details. Existing methods attempt this with expensive and narrowly-scoped video matting datasets, which may limit out-of-domain generalization and compromise tracking robustness. We rethink the paradigm with SAM2Matting, a tracker-to-matting framework that advances VOS trackers to high-fidelity video matting. Specifically, it decouples the task by enhancing a foundational tracker (e.g., SAM2, SAM3) with a region-proposal bridge and dedicated matting heads, enabling the uncompromised tracker to handle temporal consistency while the matting components resolve fine-grained details. Notably, despite being trained only on images, SAM2Matting establishes new state-of-the-art performance on video matting, supports diverse prompt types, maintains strong temporal consistency, and demonstrates robust generalization across both human-centric and in-the-wild scenarios.

</details>


### [163] [RayPE: Ray-Space Positional Encoding for 3D-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27345)
*Minghao Yin,Jiahao Lu,Wenbo Hu,Wang Zhao,Shan Ying,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RayPE，通过将6D Plücker坐标作为位置编码注入注意力机制的Q和K中，增强视频扩散Transformer的3D几何感知能力，在几乎不增加参数的情况下提升相机控制性、跨帧3D一致性和视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散Transformer仅在(u,v,t)轴上使用RoPE，这只描述了相机的采样网格，完全没有表达场景的3D结构信息。作者希望引入几何先验，让模型感知射线间的3D几何关系。

Method: 基于Plücker互异积与注意力点积在双线性形式上的类比，将每个token的6D Plücker坐标（射线方向+矩）加性注入自注意力的query和key中，并设计Q/K翻转排列使对称恒等配置恰好等于互异积。为处理不同相机平移尺度（SfM、深度SLAM、度量尺度），解耦射线方向与矩幅值，用log-幅值的门控函数控制编码，并用RMSNorm对齐QKNorm归一化的内容分支。零初始化以保持预训练权重。

Result: RayPE仅增加不到0.1%的参数，在四个数据集的混合训练中，提升了相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性和总体视频质量。消融实验表明注意力分数分解出的内容项、几何项、内容-几何交叉项均各自必要。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的几何位置编码注入，可以有效增强视频扩散模型的3D几何感知能力，且兼容不同尺度的相机位姿数据。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion transformers position their tokens through RoPE on the (u,v,t) axes -- a description of the camera's sampling grid that says nothing about the 3D structure of the scene. We observe that the geometric relation between two camera rays is captured by the Plucker reciprocal product, which is bilinear in the two rays -- the same algebraic form as the dot product in Transformer attention. Building on this analogy, we propose RayPE, a positional-encoding extension that injects per-token 6D Plucker coordinates additively into the queries and keys of self-attention, with a query/key flip arrangement under which the symmetric identity configuration coincides exactly with the reciprocal product. The injection is additive, the resulting attention score decomposes into a content term, a geometry term, and two content and geometry cross-terms -- all of which our experiments find individually necessary. To make the encoding stable across video data with heterogeneous camera-translation scales (SfM, deep SLAM, metric), we further decouple ray direction from moment magnitude, gate the encoding by a learned function of the log-magnitude, and apply RMSNorm to align it with the QKNorm-normalized content branch. The full module adds less than 0.1% parameters to a pretrained video DiT, is zero-initialized to start from the pretrained weights, and improves camera controllability, cross-frame 3D consistency, and overall video quality on a four-dataset training mixture.

</details>


### [164] [PhysiFormer: Learning to Simulate Mechanics in World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27364)
*Yiming Chen,Yushi Lan,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysiFormer是一种扩散Transformer模型，直接在3D世界坐标中对物体顶点轨迹进行去噪扩散预测，无需刚性和因果性等归纳偏置，即可生成物理上合理的刚体和弹性运动，并在轨迹精度、刚体保持和动量一致性上显著优于自回归基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在视图相关的像素空间中操作，而神经物理方法依赖于特定的潜在空间或显式施加刚性与因果性归纳偏置。作者希望直接在世界坐标系中进行顶点轨迹预测，无需这些人为偏置，并通过概率建模捕捉动力学中的不确定性。

Method: 将物体表示为3D网格（世界坐标），将顶点轨迹预测建模为单个去噪扩散过程。模型采用分解了时间、空间和物体维度的注意力机制，实现置换不变的多物体推理。输入包括初始顶点位置、速度和物体材料类型（刚性/弹性），输出未来顶点轨迹。

Result: 在超过10万条模拟轨迹上训练，能够生成刚体和弹性力学，泛化到混合材料、未见过的真实几何形状和更多物体数量的场景。在轨迹精度、刚体保持和动量一致性上大幅超越自回归基线。

Conclusion: 坐标空间扩散是迈向视角不变、几何感知的世界建模的有前景的一步，适用于机器人、图形学和物理设计。代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: We present PhysiFormer, a diffusion transformer for physically-plausible 3D object motion. Unlike video world models that operate in view-dependent pixel space, PhysiFormer represents objects as 3D meshes expressed in world coordinates. Given the initial vertex positions and velocities, as well as object material type, rigid or elastic, the model samples future vertex trajectories. While related neural physics approaches build on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicitly enforce rigidity and causality, PhysiFormer shows that excellent results can be obtained without any such inductive biases, by casting vertex trajectory prediction as a single denoising diffusion process directly in world coordinates. The probabilistic formulation captures uncertainty in the learned dynamics, enabling diverse plausible futures from initial conditions, making this framework potentially useful for applications with unobserved uncertainty. The model features attention factorised over time, space, and objects for efficiency, enabling permutation-invariant multi-object reasoning without needing explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, PhysiFormer generates rigid and elastic mechanics, and generalises to mixed-material settings, unseen real-world geometries, and larger object counts. It substantially outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and momentum-based physical consistency. Our results position coordinate-space diffusion as a promising step toward view-invariant, geometry-aware world modelling for robotics, graphics, and physical design. Visualisations, code, and models are available at https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer.

</details>


### [165] [Don't Settle at the Mode! Mitigating Diversity Collapse in Pretrained Flow Models via Feature Self-Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27371)
*Pradhaan S Bhat,Rishubh Parihar,Abhijnya Bhat,R. Venkatesh Babu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的自我引导机制，通过在批生成中分散流模型内部特征并添加流形正则化，在几乎不增加推理成本的情况下缓解条件生成中的多样性崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流模型在相同条件下生成多个样本时存在多样性崩溃问题，而现有的潜在引导方法效果有限，样本选择方法依赖外部奖励模型且推理开销大。

Method: 提出特征自我引导（feature self-guidance），在批生成过程中分散流模型的内部特征；引入流形正则化步骤，将分散的特征投影回数据流形，确保多样性同时不牺牲与输入条件的对齐。该方法即插即用，无需额外训练。

Result: 在多种条件流模型（多步/少步文生图、深度图生图、参考图生成）上显著提升了多样性，同时保持了生成保真度，且推理成本增加极小。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、无需训练、无需外部奖励模型的自我引导机制，有效解决了流模型条件生成中的多样性崩溃问题，可作为即插即用模块集成到预训练模型中。

Abstract: State-of-the-art flow models generate stunning images from text or image prompts. However, they suffer from diversity collapse when generating multiple samples under the same conditioning. Existing methods address this issue via either latent guidance, which has limited effectiveness, or sample selection, which relies on external reward models that incur significant inference-time overhead. In this work, we introduce an efficient, training-free self-guidance mechanism to mitigate diversity collapse without requiring additional reward models. Specifically, we disperse the internal features of the flow model during batch generation with feature self-guidance. Further, to keep the features close to the manifold, we introduce a manifold regularization step that projects these dispersed features back onto the data manifold, ensuring diverse generation without sacrificing alignment with the input conditions. Our method integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module into pretrained flow models, adding only a marginal inference cost. Experiments demonstrate significant improvements in diversity while preserving fidelity across several conditional flow models, including multi-step and few-step text-to-image, depth-to-image, and reference image generation.

</details>


### [166] [DnA: Denoising Attention for Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27372)
*Ron Campos,Subhajit Maity,Xin Li,Srijan Das,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Denoising Attention (DnA)，通过正负查询将特征投影到两个分离的子空间以去噪，在ImageNet-1K上提升0.8%，并在视频理解任务中取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准softmax注意力机制在视觉任务中会产生噪声注意力模式，稀释相关特征，降低模型性能。

Method: 提出Denoising Attention (DnA)：使用正查询识别正确类别的图像特征，负查询识别密切相关但不相关的干扰特征；将两种交互投影到具有更大主夹角的两个不同子空间，促进子空间分离并提升可判别性。

Result: 基于ViT-B骨干网络，在ImageNet-1K上绝对提升0.8%；视频理解任务中视频Transformer提升1.8%，视频LLM提升0.5%。

Conclusion: DnA通过正负查询的双子空间分离设计有效去噪，在多个视觉理解任务上验证了其有效性和设计合理性。

Abstract: The softmax activation in multihead attention (MHA) is the de facto standard for attention-based models in visual perception tasks. However, standard softmax can produce noisy attention patterns that dilute relevant features and degrade its performance. In this paper, we propose Denoising Attention or DnA, in which, first, a positive query identifies which image features belong to the correct class, and a negative query identifies closely associated but irrelevant image features. DnA then projects these interactions into two distinct subspaces with larger principal angles, promoting subspace separation and improved discriminability. Using a ViT-B backbone, our proposed DnA achieves an absolute gain of 0.8% on ImageNet-1K compared to the baseline. We further show improvements across multiple visual understanding tasks, including video understanding with video transformers (1.8%) and video LLMs (0.5%). Our extensive empirical analyses justify the design choices involving two interacting subspaces and the denoising effect of DnA.

</details>


### [167] [Paying More Attention to Visual Tokens in Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27373)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Ritesh Thawkar,Omkar Thawakar,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISE框架，通过几何不变性与语义不变性奖励机制，解决自进化多模态大模型中视觉条件不足的问题，在无监督设置下显著提升视觉-语言理解任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化LMMs存在视觉条件不足问题，解码器依赖语言先验而非图像内容生成输出，导致在图像描述和视觉问答等任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VISE框架，包含两种互补的不变性奖励：几何不变性奖励（强制空间一致性）和语义不变性奖励（惩罚无证据生成），在单模型内无需专家角色、外部奖励模型或标注，纯无监督训练。

Result: 在18个基准上验证，以Qwen3-VL-2B为基础模型，COCO上CIDEr提升+16.85，TextCaps上+19.66，物体幻觉降低5.0 Chair-I点，在四个模型家族和不同规模上均有泛化能力。

Conclusion: VISE通过直接正则化视觉条件策略，有效缓解了视觉条件不足问题，为自进化LMMs提供了一种无需监督的改进方案。

Abstract: Recently, self-evolving large multimodal models (LMMs) have received attention for improving visual reasoning in a purely unsupervised setting. However, multi-role self-play and self-consistency reward schemes in existing self-evolving LMMs optimize answer agreement without ensuring the decoder attends to visual content, relying instead on statistical language priors to produce self consistent outputs. This leads to a persistent failure mode we term visual under-conditioning, where the decoder relies on language priors rather than the image during generation, manifesting as insufficient attention to visual tokens. As a result, current self-evolving LMMs struggle on vision--language understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. To address this, we propose VISE (Visual Invariance Self-Evolution), a purely unsupervised self-evolving framework that directly regularizes the model's visual conditioning policy through two complementary invariance-based rewards: a geometric invariance reward that enforces spatial consistency under known transformations, and a semantic invariance reward that penalizes evidence-agnostic generation by requiring the model to recognize the absence of evidence when predicted regions are perturbed. VISE operates within a single model without specialist roles, external reward models, or annotations, and is trained on raw unlabeled images. Experiments on 18 benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of our approach. Using Qwen3-VL-2B as the base model, VISE achieves gains of $+16.85$ CIDEr on COCO and $+19.66$ CIDEr on TextCaps, reduces object hallucination by $5.0$ Chair-I points, and generalizes across four model families and scales. Our code and models are available at https://mbzuai-oryx.github.io/VISE

</details>


### [168] [Ask, Solve, Generate: Self-Evolving Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation via Self-Consistency Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27376)
*Ritesh Thawkar,Shravan Venkatraman,Omkar Thawakar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Fahad Khan,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自演进训练框架，让统一多模态大模型仅利用未标注图像自主提升视觉理解与图像生成能力，无需人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型。通过内部角色分解（提议者、求解者、生成者）与一致性信号驱动训练，在多种架构上实现理解与生成性能的共同提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态大模型仍需依赖人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型等后训练监督信号，这限制了模型自主进化能力。探索能否仅利用未标注图像实现视觉理解和图像生成能力的协同提升。

Method: 提出自演进训练框架，包含三种内部角色：(1) Proposer：生成视觉问题；(2) Solver：回答问题并评估答案质量；(3) Generator：合成图像。训练仅使用自导出的一致性信号。引入Solver Token Entropy (STE)基于词元级预测不确定性的连续难度信号，以及多尺度内部评估方案（结合问答保真度评分与循环一致性字幕生成），形成求解器介导的耦合机制。

Result: 在八项理解指标上持续优于对应基座模型；BAGEL架构上MMMU绝对提升+3.5%，GenEval图像生成性能从82%提升至85%。

Conclusion: 所提框架无需外部监督即可实现多模态能力的自主进化，且跨不同架构（扩散BLIP3o、整流流BAGEL、自回归VARGPT-v1.1）具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Most unified large multimodal models (LMMs) that support both visual understanding and image generation still rely on curated post-training supervision, such as human annotations, preference labels, or external reward models. We ask whether a unified LMM can improve both abilities autonomously using only unlabeled images. We propose a self-evolving training framework with three internal roles: a Proposer that generates visual questions, a Solver that answers and evaluates them, and a Generator that synthesizes images. Training uses only self-derived consistency signals, without human annotations, preference labels, or task-trained external reward/judge models. To stabilize learning, we introduce Solver Token Entropy (STE), a continuous difficulty signal based on token-level prediction uncertainty that remains useful even when sample-level consistency becomes unreliable. For image generation, we design a multi-scale internal evaluation scheme that combines question-answer fidelity scoring with cycle-consistent captioning. This creates a solver-mediated coupling, where better visual understanding enables more reliable generation assessment and stronger internal training signals. The framework preserves the same role decomposition, reward logic, and training schedule across diffusion-based BLIP3o, rectified-flow BAGEL, and autoregressive VARGPT-v1.1 architectures, requiring only each backbone's native prompting and generation interface. Across eight understanding metrics, our method consistently improves over the corresponding base models. On BAGEL, it achieves a $+3.5\%$ absolute gain on MMMU and improves GenEval image generation performance from $82\%$ to $85\%$. Code and models are publicly released.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [MIRAGE: Protecting against Malicious Image Editing via False Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26199)
*Anshul Nasery,Ramnath Kumar,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE方法，通过在图像中添加对抗性扰动触发商业图像编辑系统的预生成安全审核机制，从而阻止未经授权的AI图像编辑，在黑盒API上实现88%以上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像免疫方法需要访问模型权重和编辑提示词，无法应用于GPT-Image、Gemini Flash Image等闭源商业图像编辑器，亟需一种不依赖模型内部信息、提示无关的实用保护方法。

Method: 利用商业图像编辑系统的预生成安全审核这一攻击面，通过对抗性扰动将目标图像在开源嵌入和审核模型的表示空间中对齐到违反政策的概念，从而触发审核拒绝，无需知道编辑提示词。

Result: 在多个闭源图像编辑API上评估，MIRAGE成功率达到88%以上。

Conclusion: MIRAGE是一种简单、提示无关且有效的图像免疫方法，为保护个人图像免受未经授权的AI编辑提供了实用路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-powered image editing systems raises serious concerns because it allows personal images to be arbitrarily manipulated at scale, with minimal effort, and a lower barrier to entry. Prior work on image immunization adds imperceptible perturbations to an image to protect against unauthorized manipulations. However, these methods usually require access to the model weights and the image manipulating prompt. This significantly limits their use, especially against powerful commercial image-editors such as GPT-Image, Gemini Flash Image (Nano Banana), and Grok Imagine. To address this, we take a system-level view of the problem and identify a previously unexplored attack surface common to all major commercial image editing systems: pre-generation safety moderation.Rather than disrupting the generative model itself, we propose to immunize images by causing these moderation classifiers to flag images as policy-violating, triggering an automatic refusal regardless of the editing prompt. We operationalize this by adding adversarial perturbations to align our image to policy-violating concepts in the representation space of an ensemble of open-source embedding and moderation models. We call our method MIRAGE, which stands for Moderation Induced Resistance Against Generative Editing. We evaluate MIRAGE against multiple closed-source image editing APIs and demonstrate success rates of more than 88%. Our approach is simple, prompt-agnostic, and effective, offering a practical path towards protecting personal images from unauthorized AI-powered editing.

</details>


### [170] [Data Facts: A Metadata Schema for Structured Data Exchange in the NANDini Multi-Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26211)
*Jin Gao,Maria Gorskikh,Pradyumna Chari,Brittany Box,Mukul Kemla,Pratik Behera,Abhishek Mehta,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Data Facts——一种轻量级JSON元数据模式，作为NANDini自主智能体生态系统的核心组件，通过`data_facts_url`指针桥接智能体发现与数据访问，并实现三层安全流水线，大幅提升决策准确率（100% vs 35.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议（NANDA、A2A、MCP）仅支持智能体发现与通信，缺乏结构化的数据交换机制；而企业级数据共享框架（IDS-RAM、Gaia-X、Ocean Protocol）依赖人工参与，无法满足自主智能体实时交互的需求。

Method: 设计Data Facts轻量级JSON元数据模式，在Agent Facts注册记录中添加`data_facts_url`指针，对私密和半私密数据实现JWT认证、能力范围网关授权、A2A凭证委托三级安全流水线，并通过TTL新鲜度验证和SHA-256完整性校验确保数据质量。

Result: 840次决策评估中，数据驱动的智能体准确率100%（无数据访问仅35.2%，p<0.001）；TTL机制将过期数据错误从37.6%降至8.8%；校验和检测实现100%篡改检测；安全流水线阻止全部46次伪造攻击且零数据泄露。

Conclusion: Data Facts有效填补了自主智能体生态系统中结构化数据交换的空白，为物联网、边缘计算等需要实时、可信数据流的场景提供了关键基础设施支撑。

Abstract: NANDini (Networked Agents Natural Distillation of Interconnected Nodal Intelligence) envisions an automated ecosystem where intelligent agents independently create, process, and exchange data to drive decisions at scale. Realizing this vision requires infrastructure beyond agent discovery and communication: agents must be able to advertise, evaluate, and verify the datasets they hold.
  Current protocols, including NANDA for federated registry and A2A and MCP for inter-agent messaging, address identity and communication but provide no mechanism for structured data exchange. Existing enterprise data-sharing frameworks, such as IDS-RAM, Gaia-X, and Ocean Protocol, assume human-in-the-loop governance that is incompatible with autonomous, real-time agent interactions.
  We introduce Data Facts, a core NANDini concept: a lightweight JSON metadata schema that bridges agent discovery and data access through a single pointer, `data_facts_url`, added to an existing Agent Facts registry record. The linked document encodes dataset identity, access tier, whether public, semi-private, or private, endpoint, a time-to-live for freshness validation, and a SHA-256 integrity checksum.
  For private and semi-private data, we implement a three-layer security pipeline: JWT authentication, capability-scoped gateway authorization, and an A2A credential delegation protocol. Across 840 decision-making evaluations, data-informed agents achieve 100% accuracy versus 35.2% without data access (p < 0.001); TTL enforcement reduces stale-data errors from 37.6% to 8.8%; checksum verification achieves 100% corruption detection at all injection rates; and the security pipeline blocks all 46 forgery attempts with zero data leakage.

</details>


### [171] [CyberChainBench: Can AI Agents Secure Smart Contracts Against Real-World On-Chain Vulnerabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26216)
*Jintao Huang,Fengqing Jiang,Radha Poovendran,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberChainBench是一个评估LLM Agent在智能合约安全领域能力的基准，包含漏洞检测、漏洞利用和补丁合成三项互补任务，基于541个真实DeFi攻击事件构建。最佳配置在检测上得分37.5%，利用上得分43.7%，但补丁合成仅23.4%，揭示了任务难度梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约安全基准缺乏对Agent在真实链上环境中进行端到端评估的能力，且未覆盖从漏洞检测、利用到补丁合成的完整安全生命周期，无法全面衡量LLM Agent的实际安全能力。

Method: 基于DeFiHackLabs的541个真实攻击事件构建，覆盖9条EVM兼容链；通过Harbor编排隔离评估环境，Agent需与历史区块链状态交互；利用主网分叉进行漏洞利用评估，通过回放历史攻击和合法交易验证补丁有效性；定义了五种漏洞分类体系。

Result: 最佳配置（Codex+GPT-5.5）在检测、利用和补丁上的得分分别为37.5%、43.7%和23.4%；在200个利用案例中实现了总计5740万美元的利用利润，每案例成本2.39美元。

Conclusion: 智能合约安全任务的难度存在明显梯度：补丁合成最具挑战性，漏洞利用次之，漏洞检测相对最易；当前LLM Agent在真实链上环境中的安全能力仍有较大提升空间，尤其在自动化修复方面。

Abstract: We present CyberChainBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents on smart contract security across three complementary tasks: vulnerability detection, exploit generation, and patch synthesis. Built from 541 real-world exploit incidents from DeFiHackLabs spanning 9 EVM chains, the benchmark provides end-to-end on-chain evaluation where agents interact with historical blockchain state through isolated evaluation environments orchestrated by Harbor, using tools to read code, trace transactions, and validate exploits on mainnet forks. Each case is anchored to a specific block and includes structured ground truth covering vulnerability type, localization, and attacker profit. Exploits are graded by economic impact on historical forks; patches are validated by replaying historical attacks and legitimate transactions as fail-to-pass test oracles on a proxy-upgradeable subset. We define a five-type vulnerability taxonomy and evaluate multiple agent--model configurations. Results reveal a clear difficulty gradient: the best configuration scores 37.5% on detection, 43.7% on exploitation, but only 23.4% on patching, with the top agent (Codex with GPT-5.5) realizing \$57.4M in total exploit profit across the 200-case exploit set at a cost of $2.39 per case.

</details>


### [172] [Expecting (Targeted Ads)? Network Analysis of User Health Data Leakage in Fertility Tracking Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26276)
*Yeeun Jo,Shahanaasree Sivakumar,Mahnoor Jameel,Camille Cobb,Adam Bates,Brad Reaves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对20款Android生育追踪应用进行网络测量，发现部分应用存在用户健康数据泄露（显式泄露和通过上下文广告的隐式泄露），但也有一些采用广告变现模式的应用并未泄露用户数据，强调了用户选择在隐私保护中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生育追踪应用的用户隐私因素已被广泛研究，但应用数据处理实践的技术层面（尤其是与第三方广告服务的数据共享机制）尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 对Google Play商店中20款Android生育追踪应用进行基于网络的测量研究；系统化应用功能后，在记录TLS剥离网络流量的环境中执行标准化用户交互；分析网络流量中用户数据向第三方广告服务的共享情况。

Result: 在5款应用中检测到明确的用户健康数据泄露（显式泄露）以及通过高度定向的上下文广告URL的隐式泄露；同时观察到部分应用采用广告变现模型但无明显数据泄露，以及一些应用与广告服务交互极少。

Conclusion: 研究为用户的广泛隐私担忧提供了技术依据，同时强调了在基于应用的生育追踪中，消费者选择对隐私影响的重要作用。

Abstract: While human factors in the privacy of fertility tracking apps -- health trackers that record user's menstrual or pregnancy data -- has been the subject of extensive study, little attention has been paid to the technical aspects of apps' data handling practices. We conduct a network-based measurement study of a corpus of 20 Android fertility tracking apps from the Google Play Store, focusing on how user data is shared with third party advertising services. After systematizing app features, we conduct a series of standardized user interactions across all apps in an environment that records TLS-stripped network traffic. In a subset of apps (n=5) we identify explicit leakage of user health data as well implicit leakage through highly targeted contextual advertising URL's. Equally importantly, we observe additional apps that use an ad-based monetization model without apparent leakage of user data, as well as several apps the interact only minimally with ad services. These findings provide technical grounding for widespread user concerns, but also underscore the importance of consumer choice in the privacy implications of app-based fertility tracking.

</details>


### [173] [TEMPO-Diffusion: Temporally Exposed Malicious Poisoning of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26285)
*William Aiken,Paula Branco,Guy-Vincent Jourdan,Iosif-Viorel Onut*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TEMPO-Diffusion是一个针对扩散模型的时序条件触发后门攻击框架，能够实现类特定的靶向攻击、多子图像多位置后门嵌入以及基于时序触发器的图像修复。通过CALISA数据集验证，该方法能有效毒化类特定合成数据生成，并在下游分类器上实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于噪声的后门攻击扩散模型依赖输入时触发注入、非靶向激活和分布外目标生成，这些假设降低了攻击的隐蔽性和实际相关性。作者旨在设计更具隐蔽性和实用性的靶向后门攻击框架。

Method: 提出TEMPO-Diffusion框架，将恶意分布偏移定位到时序、分布内暴露中。支持：(i) 针对特定类别的靶向攻击，(ii) 多子图像后门以重构多个不同输出图像中多个位置的特定特征，(iii) 结合时序条件触发器的图像修复。同时引入CALISA数据集用于评估。

Result: 在CIFAR10、GTSRB和CALISA数据集上的实验表明，TEMPO-Diffusion能够可靠地毒化类特定合成数据生成，并在基于该数据训练的下游分类器上诱导高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: TEMPO-Diffusion为扩散模型提供了一种更隐蔽、更实用的靶向后门攻击方法，能够有效利用被后门化的扩散模型进行合成训练数据的毒化攻击。

Abstract: Noise-based backdoor attacks on diffusion models typically rely on input-time trigger injection, untargeted activation, and out-of-distribution target generation. Such assumptions reduce both the stealthiness and the practical relevance of these attacks. In this work, we present TEMPO-Diffusion, a targeted backdoor framework that localizes the malicious distribution shift to a temporal, in-distribution exposure. TEMPO-Diffusion supports: (i) targeted attacks on and to specific classes, (ii) multiple sub-image backdoors that reconstruct specific features within multiple, different output images and at multiple locations, and (iii) in-painting with time-conditioned triggers. To study relevant, practical security concerns in leveraging backdoored diffusion models for synthetic training data, we also introduce CALISA: a balanced, region-aware traffic-sign dataset emphasizing Canadian and U.S. road signs. Across CIFAR10, GTSRB, and CALISA, our experiments show that TEMPO-Diffusion can reliably poison class-specific synthetic data generation and induce high attack success rates in downstream classifiers trained on that data.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Takedown: Measuring Malicious Go Module Persistence in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26291)
*Minjae Bae,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了Go生态系统中的自动化供应链攻击，发现2,289个恶意模块版本，揭示了GitHub中心化搜索的局限性，并指出99.4%的恶意模块即使在GitHub上被移除后仍可通过Go代理获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go生态系统中的自动化供应链攻击日益严重，攻击者通过重新打包合法Go模块并嵌入混淆代码实施攻击，需要系统性地测量和揭示这种威胁的规模和特性。

Method: 采用两种互补分析方法：a) 手动搜索GitHub上的2,113个仓库；b) 使用实现的去混淆AST扫描器（GOAST）对12.3M索引条目进行大规模扫描。

Result: 识别出2,289个恶意版本的合法Go模块；发现纯GitHub中心化搜索无法识别全部威胁；在GitHub上不可观察的恶意工件中，至少99.4%仍可通过Go代理检索；GitHub已移除684个恶意仓库，Google Go团队已修复1,377个模块版本。

Conclusion: 纯GitHub中心化的安全检测存在严重局限性，Go代理的缓存机制导致移除-修复差距巨大，需要更全面的供应链安全检测与响应机制。

Abstract: We measure an automation-based supply chain campaign in the Go ecosystem. The attackers repackage legitimate Go modules under attacker-controlled owners, and embed them with obfuscated code for an import-triggered downloader. Our results come from two complementary analyses: a) a manual search on GitHub across 2,113 repositories and b) a large-scale scan of 12.3M index entries using a deobfuscating AST scanner (GOAST) that we implemented. As a result, we identified 2,289 malicious versions of legitimate Go modules. We demonstrate that purely GitHub-centric searches fail to identify the full extent of the compromise and are only effective for as long as the affected code is present on the platform. Moreover, our proxy-based measurements of the takedown-remediation gap reveal that among artifacts later found to be GitHub-unobservable (i.e., removed or suspended), at least 99.4% remained retrievable via Go proxy. Following our disclosure, GitHub has removed 684 malicious repositories and the Google Go team has remediated 1,377 module versions.

</details>


### [175] [Hybrid privacy-aware semantic search: SVD-truncated document geometry and CKKS-encrypted query reranking under a restricted threat model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26373)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合保护方案：文档向量通过SVD截断+秘密旋转进行几何保护，查询向量通过CKKS同态加密进行密码学保护。在百万文档规模下保持亚秒级延迟和排序质量，但文档保护属于经验性混淆而非加密原语，并严格限定了威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御极端：同态加密安全但太慢，隐私噪声保护不足。需要一种在效率与安全性之间的折中方案，利用静态集合与动态查询的不对称性。

Method: 集合端：向量经SVD降维截断到低维子空间，再施以仅所有者知晓的秘密正交变换。查询端：使用CKKS同态加密进行重排序，服务器无法看到查询向量或分数。CKKS参数通过离线小规模基准测试确定。

Result: 在百万文档和五种编码器上，方案保持（甚至略有提升）排序质量，延迟亚秒级；现成反演攻击在保护空间内崩溃至噪声底限；已知明文攻击需泄露约等于保留维度的配对数量才能恢复旋转；PQ编码保留大部分最近邻结构。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了可操作的折中：查询机密性是密码学级，但文档保护是经验性混淆层（SVD截断+秘密旋转），非密码学原语。论文为每项声明划定了威胁模型边界。

Abstract: Dense embeddings power semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation, but embedding-inversion attacks can reconstruct source text from a vector: when a vector database leaks, the documents behind it leak too. The textbook defences are extremes - encrypting the whole search homomorphically is sound but too slow at million-document scale, while privacy noise degrades ranking long before it protects. We study a middle path exploiting the asymmetry between the static collection and the dynamic query. The collection is protected geometrically: each vector is truncated onto a lower-dimensional SVD subspace and rotated by a secret orthogonal transform known only to the owner. The query is protected cryptographically: it is reranked under CKKS homomorphic encryption, so an honest-but-curious server never sees the query or the scores. CKKS parameters come from a small offline benchmark.
  We prove a tight lower bound on the reconstruction error of any attacker confined to the protected subspace. On one million documents and five encoders the scheme preserves ranking quality (slightly improving it on strong encoders, as a linear denoiser) at sub-second latency, and an off-the-shelf inversion attack on the protected space collapses to the noise floor. We then test stronger adversaries: a known-plaintext attacker recovers the rotation by orthogonal Procrustes from about as many leaked pairs as the retained dimension; the public product-quantization codes preserve most nearest-neighbour structure; and random-projection, calibrated-noise and BEIR baselines show the truncation is an encoder-dependent accuracy cost, not a free denoiser. We state the limits: query confidentiality is cryptographic, but document protection is an empirical obfuscation layer (SVD truncation plus a secret rotation), not a cryptographic primitive, and we delimit the threat model for each claim.

</details>


### [176] [Verifying Intent and Harm: A Unified Defense Against LLM-Generated Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26377)
*Poojitha Thota,Yun Lei,Santhosh Thangaraj,Siddhartha Reddy Jonnalagadda,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种联合验证提示意图和响应危害的防御框架，在五个威胁类别上实现平均F1 0.95、攻击成功率4.1%，优于单侧防御和单智能体推理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御仅分析用户提示或生成输出中的一方，但许多真实攻击利用提示中的恶意意图与响应中的可操作危害之间的分离，导致单侧防御经常遗漏看似良性的不安全交互。

Method: 提出验证中心防御框架，包含意图分析师（评估提示）、危害分析师（评估响应）和法官（冲突解决），在响应交付给用户前联合验证提示意图和响应危害。

Result: 在越狱、提示注入、钓鱼、网络滥用和有害内容五个类别上，平均F1从强基线的0.90提升至0.95，平均攻击成功率降至4.1%；相比单智能体+CoT基线，F1从0.87提升至0.95，假阳性率从0.12降至0.06。

Conclusion: 提示-响应联合验证为保护LLM应用免受不断演变的对抗性威胁提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive applications, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial interactions that induce harmful, deceptive, or policy-violating outputs. Existing defenses typically analyze either user prompts or generated outputs, but not both. However, many real-world attacks exploit a separation between adversarial intent expressed in the prompt and actionable harm manifested only in the response. As a result, prompt-only and response-only defenses frequently miss unsafe interactions that appear benign when viewed from either side in isolation. We present a verification-centric defense framework that jointly evaluates prompt intent and response harm before an LLM response is delivered to a user. The framework employs specialized analysts for intent and harm assessment together with a Judge for conflict resolution. We formalize a threat model for prompt-response attacks and evaluate the framework across five threat categories: jailbreaks, prompt injection, phishing, cyber abuse, and harmful content. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that jointly verifying prompt intent and response harm consistently outperforms single-sided defenses and single-agent reasoning baselines. Across threat categories, the framework improves average F1 from 0.90 for the strongest applicable baselines to 0.95 while reducing the average attack success rate to 4.1 percent. Compared with a Single-Agent+CoT baseline, it improves average F1 from 0.87 to 0.95 and reduces the false positive rate on benign-sensitive requests from 0.12 to 0.06. We further evaluate architecture-aware adaptive attacks in which the attacker knows the verifier structure and attempts to bypass individual verification components. Our results suggest that prompt-response verification provides a practical foundation for securing LLM applications against evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [177] [Lessons from the Adoption and Deprecation of the Privacy Sandbox Web APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26390)
*Yohan Beugin,Paul Barford,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对Privacy Sandbox API的七年纵向测量研究，分析了其采用率始终有限且不均衡，最终于2025年被Google取消的原因，并提出了对下一代网络隐私提案的可行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多年来多个网络参与者试图减少网络追踪，但如何在效用和隐私之间取得理想平衡仍不明确。Google于2019年启动Privacy Sandbox计划以解决这一权衡问题，但该计划于2025年底被取消。本文旨在利用这一独特机会，通过纵向测量分析生态系统对拟议变更的反应，探究该计划失败的原因和方式。

Method: 利用历史HTTP Archive爬虫数据和公开的Chrome遥测数据，对每个Privacy Sandbox功能在整个生命周期（部分超过5年）内，在流行网站（CrUX前10万）及Chrome用户浏览旅程中的采用情况进行大规模纵向测量与分析。

Result: 结果显示，Privacy Sandbox API的采用率始终有限且不均衡；只有少数网络参与者实施了非常特定的API，且实施方式各不相同。此外，Chrome中的追踪和第三方Cookie限制在很大程度上仍是选择加入的，而Brave、Firefox、Safari等其他浏览器已默认启用这些限制。

Conclusion: 基于对不同参与者的激励因素（兴趣、资源、时间线等）和风险（潜在权衡、隐私侵犯、法律风险等）的考量来解释上述结果。同时提出对下一代网络隐私提案的可行建议。更广泛地揭示了"在浏览器中修复"方案的局限性和跨浏览器差异。

Abstract: While several web actors have been trying to reduce web tracking for years, it remains unclear how to achieve both desirable levels of utility and privacy. In 2019, Google launched the Privacy Sandbox initiative to balance that trade-off and find privacy alternatives to common use cases such as advertising. Yet, in late 2025, Google canceled the project and deprecated most of the newly introduced APIs. Despite its end, the Privacy Sandbox represents a unique opportunity to learn about how the ecosystem reacted to the proposed changes and make observations about why and how it failed. In this paper, we present a longitudinal measurement and analysis study of the Privacy Sandbox APIs to characterize their adoption and deprecation over the past seven years by different web actors. Leveraging historical HTTP Archive crawls and public Chrome telemetry data, we offer the largest study of its kind into the prevalence of each Privacy Sandbox feature, during their entire respective lifetime (5+ years for some), on popular websites (CrUX top 100k), and as experienced by Chrome users during their browsing journey. Our results showcase an adoption that remained limited and uneven across the years; only few web actors implemented very specific APIs, and in disparate manners. We motivate our interpretation of these results by considering the incentives (interest, resources, timeline, etc.) and risks (potential trade-offs, privacy violations, and legal exposure, etc.) for these actors. Finally, our analysis also yields actionable recommendations for the next generation of web privacy proposals. More broadly, the Privacy Sandbox illustrates the limitations and disparities across browsers of ``fix it in the browser'' remedies: today, tracking and third-party cookies limitations in Chrome still remain largely opt-in, while they have been enabled by default on other browsers like Brave, Firefox, or Safari.

</details>


### [178] [What Browsers Do in the Shaders: A Measurement Study of WebGPU Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26412)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出WGPULens框架，系统测量WebGPU在浏览器中的隐私泄露风险。研究发现管道编译状态是最清晰的隐私暴露面，冷/热管道探测可揭示跨源、跨配置文件的编译状态；原生GPU活动也可通过浏览器可见信号推断。现场研究表明WebGPU行为具有高度区分性，网页爬虫发现WebGPU主要用于适配器探测而非实际计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU通过验证编程模型保障内存安全，但共享的浏览器、驱动、操作系统及GPU状态仍可能暴露隐私相关信号，现有研究对此缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 构建WGPULens框架，包含三个维度：(1) 受控场景实验（泄露边界、缓解策略测试）；(2) 参与者现场研究（部署兼容性、指纹识别性分析）；(3) Tranco网站爬虫（真实WebGPU暴露情况）。

Result: (1) 管道编译状态是最清晰的泄露面，冷/热探测可揭示跨源、跨配置文件的先前编译状态；(2) 浏览器/原生混合实验中，可从浏览器可见信号推断原生GPU活动；(3) 参与者研究中WebGPU行为具有高度区分性，确定性组件在单次运行内稳定但跨次访问稳定性较低；(4) 网页爬虫发现WebGPU主要用于适配器探测和静态支持代码，未观察到实际计算活动。

Conclusion: WebGPU隐私分析必须针对具体暴露面：浏览器需测量哪些GPU状态跨越哪些边界、哪些浏览器可见信号揭示这些状态、以及相应缓解措施的成本。源级密钥隔离可作为管道缓存分区评估的代理方案。

Abstract: WebGPU lets ordinary web pages run GPU workloads through a validated programming model. Validation protects memory safety, but shared browser, driver, OS, and GPU state can still expose privacy-relevant signals. We present WGPULens, a framework for measuring those signals across controlled scenarios, browser-native co-residency, a participant field study, public page loads, and mitigation policies. Our framework separates measurements: controlled scenarios support leakage, boundary, and mitigation claims; participant runs support deployment, compatibility, and fingerprintability; and a Tranco crawl measures WebGPU exposure in real-world pages.
  Our controlled results identify persistent pipeline compilation state as the clearest surface. Cold/warm pipeline probes reveal prior compilation state across selected origin, profile, and browser placements. Controlled browser/native experiments also show native GPU activity can be inferred from browser-visible observables under labeled workloads. Other resource probes provide weaker positive results and negative controls.
  The participant field study shows active WebGPU behavior is highly distinctive within the sample, with deterministic components stable within runs and lower exact stability across repeated visits. A page-load crawl finds WebGPU use mainly as adapter probing and static support code, with no observed page-load shader, pipeline, queue, query, or map activity. Mitigation pilots identify source-level key separation as a proxy for evaluating pipeline-cache partitioning. Overall, WGPULens shows that WebGPU privacy analysis must be surface-specific: browsers need to measure which GPU state crosses which boundary, which browser-visible signals reveal it, and what the corresponding mitigations cost.

</details>


### [179] [Adaptive Evaluation of Out-of-Band Defenses Against Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26479)
*Praneeth Narisetty,Shiva Nagendra Babu Kore,Uday Kumar Reddy Kattamanchi,Jayaram Kumarapu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对工具型LLM代理的间接提示注入攻击，近期工作（2024-2026）提出了一种新的防御策略：不在模型内部训练拒绝恶意指令的能力，而是在模型外部通过确定性策略强制执行安全控制。本文首先将这些"带外防御"系统（CaMeL、FIDES、Progent、RTBAS、FORGE）组织为经典完整性保护（Biba模型）、引用监控器和最小权限原则的实例，进行了结构化比较。其次，本文警告所有这些防御仅在静态基准上验证，而相同的方法论曾让带内防御看起来很强，直到自适应、感知防御的攻击以超过90%的成功率攻破了十二个此类系统。本文随后在AgentDojo上使用开源模型（Qwen2.5-7B）自托管在单张H200上，独立复现并扩展了Progent的自适应攻击分析。三次运行平均结果显示：Progent将平均攻击成功率从25.8%降至4.2%（约六倍降低），而手工定制的自适应攻击并未提高成功率（2.6%）。这是一个弱模型上的小规模数据点，更强的优化攻击（白盒GCG）仍有待探索。该结果与"确定性带外强制比带内检测更难被自适应攻击者攻破"的假设一致，但尚未确立该假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
