Motivation: 现有针对工具型LLM代理的间接提示注入防御（带内防御）已被自适应攻击以超过90%成功率攻破。近期提出的带外防御虽报告了优异性能，但仅在静态基准上验证，可能面临同样的方法论缺陷。本文旨在系统比较这些带外防御的结构差异，并通过严格的自适应评估协议检验其鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 将CaMeL、FIDES、Progent、RTBAS、FORGE等带外防御系统统一到Biba完整性保护、引用监控器和最小权限原则的理论框架下进行结构化比较。2) 明确自适应评估所需的威胁模型和评估协议。3) 在AgentDojo基准上，使用开源模型Qwen2.5-7B自托管在单张H200上，独立复现并扩展Progent的自适应攻击分析（三次运行平均）。

Result: Progent将平均攻击成功率从25.8%降至4.2%（约六倍降低），手工定制的自适应攻击未提高成功率（2.6%）。但该结果是弱模型上的小规模数据点，白盒GCG等更强优化攻击仍有待测试。

Conclusion: 该实验数据与"确定性带外强制比带内检测更难被自适应攻击者攻破"的假设一致，但尚不能确立该假设。需要更全面的自适应评估（包括白盒优化攻击）来验证带外防御的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent work (2024 to 2026) has converged on a strategy for defending tool-using LLM agents against indirect prompt injection: rather than training the model to refuse malicious instructions, enforce security outside the model with a deterministic policy that mediates the agent's actions. Systems such as CaMeL, FIDES, Progent, RTBAS, and FORGE realize this with capabilities, information-flow labels, and reference monitors, and several report near-elimination of attacks on the AgentDojo benchmark. We make two contributions. First, we organize these out-of-band defenses as instances of classical integrity protection (Biba), reference monitoring, and least privilege, yielding a structured comparison of what they do and do not cover. Second, we warn that every one of them is validated only on static benchmarks (a fixed set of injection attempts), the same methodology that made in-band defenses look strong until adaptive, defense-aware attacks broke twelve of them at over 90% success; we specify the threat model and protocol an adaptive evaluation requires. We then run that protocol as an independent reproduction and extension of Progent's own adaptive-attack analysis, on AgentDojo, with an open-weight agent (Qwen2.5-7B) self-hosted on a single H200, a setting its authors did not test. Averaged over three runs, the defense held: Progent cut mean attack success roughly sixfold (25.8% to 4.2%), and a hand-crafted adaptive attack did not raise it (2.6%). This is one small-scale data point on a weak model with a single black-box attack template; a stronger optimized (white-box GCG) attack remains open. The result is consistent with, but does not establish, the hypothesis that deterministic out-of-band enforcement is a harder target for an adaptive attacker than in-band detection.

</details>


### [180] [DKVE: Decentralized Key Validation for End-to-End Encrypted Messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26486)
*Subin Song,Taekyoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DKVE协议通过隐私保护的社交图交叉验证来检测端到端加密消息系统中的中间人攻击，在强到中等关系网络中检出率超过97%，可将密钥透明度查询频率降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端加密消息系统的公钥分发存在两难困境：带外验证安全性强但不可扩展，密钥透明度系统可实现自动化验证但存储成本和操作复杂度过高。需要一种兼顾安全性与可扩展性的公钥验证机制。

Method: 提出DKVE协议，利用社交图中共同联系人进行隐私保护的交叉验证。客户端获取目标公钥后，通过OPRF和OKVS技术向共同联系人查询密钥的一致性，在保护查询隐私和联系人列表隐私的同时，使用序贯概率比检验聚合响应并检测服务器异常行为。

Result: 在真实社交网络数据集上，DKVE对强到中等关系网络的MitM攻击检出率超过97%，剩余的3%需通过KT或OOB等替代方法验证。原型实现证实可在普通硬件上作为后台任务运行，并将KT查询频率降低两个数量级。

Conclusion: DKVE无法完全替代现有方法（存在冷启动问题，在弱关系网络中性能下降），但提供了实用的互补性密钥验证机制，支持KT目录从Merkle树迁移到RSA累加器等空间高效数据结构，使安全消息系统更易于在十亿用户规模下部署。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted messaging systems depend on authentic public key distribution to prevent man-in-the-middle (MitM) attacks. Current solutions present a stark trade-off: out-of-band (OOB) verification provides strong security but lacks scalability for large contact lists, while key transparency (KT) systems enable automated verification at high storage costs and operational complexity.
  We propose DKVE, a protocol that validates public keys through privacy-preserving cross-validation within users' social graphs. When obtaining a contact's public key from a key server, clients query mutual contacts to verify they hold the same key, combining Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions (OPRF) and Oblivious Key-Value Stores (OKVS) to preserve privacy of both queries and contact lists. DKVE employs a Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT) to aggregate responses and detect server misbehavior with user-configurable error bounds.
  We evaluate DKVE through simulations on real social network datasets, demonstrating DKVE can detect MitM attacks with exceeding 97% for strong-to-moderate-tie networks. The remaining 3% of cases require validation through alternative methods such as KT and OOB verification. Our proof-of-concept implementation confirms feasibility for background operation on commodity hardware, in terms of the latency and bandwidth.
  As DKVE can reduce the frequency of KT queries by two orders of magnitude, it enables fundamental architectural shifts: KT directories can migrate from fast but space-inefficient Merkle trees to space-efficient data structures like RSA accumulators. While DKVE cannot replace existing methods entirely -- suffering from bootstrapping problems and degraded performance on weak-tie networks -- it provides a practical complementary key validation mechanism, making secure messaging more deployable for billion-user systems.

</details>


### [181] [VIGIL: Runtime Enforcement of Behavioral Specifications in AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26524)
*Ying Li,Yanju Chen,Hongbo Wen,Bosi Zhang,Hanzhi Liu,Peiran Wang,Yu Feng,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VIGIL是一个面向智能体系统的端到端运行时强制框架，通过将自然语言策略规范转化为SMT约束，在细粒度层面检测跨工具调用的事件顺序、参数关系和值流违规，在多个LLM智能体任务上实现了超过95%的召回率和低于10%的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统依赖第三方技能，其自然语言权限规范缺乏可执行运行时强制能力；而现有强制机制采用固定事件模型或强制点，无法解决上下文粒度挑战——即如何在正确的事件粒度上进行观察、状态保留、推理和干预，避免误阻断合法执行或遗漏跨多个动作的违规。

Method: VIGIL提出了一个策略语言，用于捕获智能体-工具事件上的上下文特定强制需求（包括时间依赖、参数约束和值流条件）；配合符号评估规则将策略转化为有限迹上的SMT约束，从而检测依赖事件顺序、参数关系或跨调用值流的违规行为。

Result: 在办公室文档、运维和工程任务等真实LLM智能体运行轨迹上，VIGIL检测策略违规的召回率超过95%，假阳性率低于10%。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过细粒度的符号约束求解方法，有效解决了智能体系统中策略强制面临的上下文粒度挑战，为多技能交互场景下的运行时安全监控提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly act through third-party skills, allowing model-generated decisions to affect files, communication channels, and cyber-physical devices. These skills often include natural-language specifications that define access permissions, disclosure limits, execution privileges, and required preconditions. Although such specifications describe the intended boundaries of skill behavior, they do not by themselves provide executable runtime enforcement. Enforcing them raises a contextual granularity challenge: even when a policy is written for a particular task context, a monitor must still decide which events to observe, what state to retain, how far across the execution to reason, and where to intervene. Choosing the wrong granularity can either block benign executions or miss violations that emerge only across multiple actions. Most existing enforcement mechanisms, however, assume a fixed event model or enforcement point.
  In this work, we present VIGIL, an end-to-end runtime enforcement framework for agentic systems. VIGIL checks an agent's actual execution trace against behavioral policies from skill specifications, operator-defined constraints, and global rules spanning multiple skills. To make such policies executable, VIGIL introduces a policy language that captures context-specific enforcement requirements over agent-tool events, including temporal dependencies, argument constraints, and value-flow conditions. The language is paired with symbolic evaluation rules that translate policies into SMT constraints over finite traces, allowing VIGIL to detect violations that depend on event order, argument relationships, or cross-call value flow rather than relying on fixed single-call filters. On real LLM-agent runs spanning office-document, operational, and engineering tasks, VIGIL detects policy violations with over 95% recall and a false-positive rate below 10%.

</details>


### [182] [TESLA-for-5G: Broadcast Authentication for 5G Networks Using TESLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26528)
*Subin Song,Michael K. Reiter,Taekyoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TESLA-for-5G (TF5)，一种结合TESLA与GG09身份基签名的5G SIB1广播认证协议，在稳态使用对称MAC和延迟密钥披露实现轻量级认证，初始信任通过轻量级IBS引导，经形式化验证和实际部署评估成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G基站广播的SI未经认证，易受伪基站伪造SIB1攻击；现有基于数字签名的方案每次SIB1接收都需要计算密集型验证，对资源受限的UE造成沉重负担。

Method: 将TESLA广播认证协议与GG09 Schnorr类身份基签名(IBS)相结合：稳态下使用对称MAC和延迟密钥披露认证每条SIB1；初始信任通过轻量级GG09 IBS在小区接入时引导TESLA参数，避免证书分发开销。

Result: 在Tamarin下完成了对Dolev-Yao敌手的形式化安全验证；通过在OpenAirInterface 5G协议栈上的实现和基于真实数据的trace驱动分析，展示了其在计算、通信和存储成本方面的优势。

Conclusion: TF5在保持安全性的同时，显著降低了UE在SIB1认证上的计算开销，是替代现有数字签名方案的有效轻量级方案。

Abstract: 5G base stations broadcast unauthenticated system information (SI) that every user equipment (UE) reads during cell selection. This enables attackers to broadcast forged SI from a fake base station (FBS), deceiving UEs into camping on it. Prior approaches require UEs to authenticate System Information Block 1 (SIB1) using digital signatures. This necessitates computation-heavy verification for every SIB1 reception, imposing a significant burden on resource-constrained UEs. We propose TESLA-for-5G (TF5), a broadcast authentication protocol for 5G SIB1 that combines TESLA with GG09 Schnorr-like identity-based signatures (IBS). In the steady state, TF5 enables UEs to authenticate each SIB1 message using a symmetric MAC and delayed key disclosure, eliminating the need for per-message digital signatures. Initial trust is bootstrapped during cell entry using a lightweight GG09 IBS over the TESLA parameters, avoiding certificate distribution overhead. We formally verify TF5 in Tamarin under a Dolev-Yao adversary and demonstrate its favorable computation, communication, and storage costs through both an implementation on the OpenAirInterface 5G stack and trace-driven analysis.

</details>


### [183] [Adversarial Diffusion Across Modalities: A Fusion Survey of Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation for Text, Vision, and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26566)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对AI系统对抗性评估中四个分散的研究轨道（文本/LLM扩散攻击、图像分类器扩散攻击、视觉语言模型越狱流水线、扩散输入净化防御）进行了元层面的信息融合，构建了统一的概念框架、分类法和研究议程，并针对LLM侧进行了重点分析。文章收录50篇论文，提出六类扩散角色分类法、威胁模型轴和五维评估框架，从攻防双视角批判了当前LLM侧文献的五项弱点，并给出了开放性问题和实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四个研究轨道各自发展出独立的词汇体系、威胁模型和基准测试，缺乏统一的元研究框架来整合这些分散的成果。扩散模型作为共享的生成机制，其配方正在不同社区之间迁移，亟需一个跨轨道的统一分类、评估标准和研究议程。

Method: 采用元研究层面的信息融合方法，整合50篇已发表论文（涵盖文本/LLM、图像分类器、视觉语言模型、防御四大范围，外加4篇扩散-LLM作为受害者条目和10个非扩散基线），提出六类扩散角色分类法、威胁模型轴（攻击者知识、查询预算、目标可访问性）和五维评估框架（攻击成功率、迁移性、查询预算、困惑度、防御绕过）。

Result: 构建了统一的概念框架和分类体系；识别出当前LLM侧文献的五项重复性弱点；发布了配套的目录和电子表格；明确指出这是一项带有质量评估的叙述性综述而非PRISMA合规的系统性综述。

Conclusion: 本文为AI系统对抗性评估中四个分散轨道提供了首个元层面的整合框架，以LLM侧为焦点呈现了攻防双视角的分析，并提出了未来研究的开放性问题和具体实验设计，为后续研究提供了统一的参考基准和研究路线图。

Abstract: Adversarial evaluation of AI systems has matured along four largely disconnected tracks: diffusion-based attacks on text and large language models (LLMs), diffusion-based attacks on image classifiers, jailbreak pipelines against vision-language models, and diffusion-based input purification defenses. Each has developed its own vocabulary, threat models, and benchmarks, with denoising diffusion models emerging as a shared generative mechanism whose recipes are now actively ported between communities. This survey performs an information-fusion exercise at the meta-research level: we integrate these four tracks into a single conceptual framework with a unified taxonomy, evaluation criteria, and research agenda, focusing on the LLM-side slice. We catalog fifty published papers across four scope areas (text/LLM, image classifier, vision-language model, defense), plus four diffusion-LLM-as-victim entries and ten non-diffusion baselines against which any new attack must be compared. We propose a six-class taxonomy of diffusion roles in adversarial pipelines, augmented by a threat-model axis recording attacker knowledge, query budget, and target accessibility, and apply a five-dimension framework (attack success rate, transferability, query budget, perplexity, defense-evasion) uniformly across modalities. The review adopts a dual attacker-defender perspective: alongside the attack catalog we cover four diffusion-based defenses that form the natural evaluation backdrop for new attacks. Our critical analysis identifies five recurring weaknesses of the current LLM-side literature, and we close with a research agenda of open questions and concrete experimental designs. The companion catalog and spreadsheet are released with the paper. We are explicit that this is a narrative review with quality assessment, not a PRISMA-compliant systematic review, and discuss the implications for replication.

</details>


### [184] [Agents That Know Too Much: A Data-Centric Survey of Privacy in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26627)
*Nada Lahjouji,Ashwin Gerard Colaco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从数据中心的视角系统综述了大语言模型智能体的隐私风险，分类梳理了智能体接触的数据源、各类隐私风险及治理机制，发现信息流控制是唯一能同时覆盖组合推理和跨会话推理泄露的治理机制，且目前缺乏一个统一的隐私策略基准来驱动智能体跨数据面测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在查询数据库、调用外部API、跨会话记忆和代理用户操作时，涉及多源数据和多步工作流，传统隐私保护方法难以适用。现有研究分散在RAG、Text-to-SQL、智能体记忆、提示注入、访问控制等不同领域，缺乏统一框架来系统理解智能体面临的隐私风险。

Method: 采用数据中心的分类视角，以智能体接触的数据源为核心组织文献，而非按攻击类型划分。通过系统梳理智能体涉及的数据源、每种数据源对应的隐私风险、现有的治理机制以及评测基准，建立统一的分类体系。

Result: (1) 识别出智能体隐私泄露的多个途径：最终回答、查询语句、中间结果、记忆写回、智能体间消息； (2) 在治理机制中，只有信息流控制能同时覆盖组合推理泄露和跨会话推理泄露这两个最缺乏保护的风险； (3) 目前没有一个基准能在统一隐私策略下驱动智能体跨越所有数据面进行测试，这是该领域最缺乏的工具。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一框架来组织分散的文献，为未来研究提供共同参照体系。两个关键开放问题是：如何设计覆盖组合和跨会话推理的信息流控制机制，以及如何构建统一的跨数据面隐私策略基准。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly query databases, search document collections, call external APIs, remember past interactions, and act on a user's behalf. As they move from answering questions to operating over sensitive data, privacy becomes harder to enforce. An agent touches many data sources, runs multi-step workflows, keeps state across sessions, and acts with delegated permissions. Sensitive information can therefore leak not only through its final answer but through the queries it issues, the intermediate results it handles, the memory it writes, and the messages it exchanges with other agents. We survey the privacy of LLM agents from a data-centric view, organizing the field around the data an agent touches rather than by attack type, and we use data agent as shorthand for an LLM agent that works with data. Research on these risks is active but scattered across retrieval-augmented generation, text-to-SQL interfaces, agent memory, prompt injection, access control, and contextual privacy. This survey brings that work together: we taxonomize the data sources an agent touches, the privacy risks each source creates, and the governance mechanisms that address them; we map the benchmarks used to measure these risks and identify what is missing; and we set out the open problems. Two findings recur: among governance mechanisms only information-flow control covers both compositional and cross-session inference leakage, the two least-protected risks; and no benchmark drives an agent across its data surfaces under one privacy policy, the instrument the field most lacks. Our goal is a reference that situates the scattered literature and gives future work a common framing.

</details>


### [185] [TGHE: Template-based Graph Homomorphic Encryption for Privacy-Preserving GNN Inference in Edge-Cloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26664)
*Ngoc Bao Anh Le,Thai T. Vu,John Le,Heath Cooper,Jun Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGHE提出了一种基于模板的图同态加密框架，利用交易图中局部计算树的结构趋同现象，将同构的ego-graph打包到共享CKKS密文中进行SIMD并行加密推理，在DGraphFin（370万节点）上相比顺序加密基线实现66.9倍加速，AUC损失小于0.002。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于同态加密的GNN系统采用图中心范式，将每次查询的成本与全局图大小耦合，最多只能处理约2万个节点，无法应用于动态、大规模金融图。

Method: 提出TGHE框架：（1）利用交易图中局部计算树收敛为少量结构形状的模板现象；（2）在边层对ego-graph进行规范化，将结构相同的树打包到共享CKKS密文中实现SIMD并行加密推理；（3）设计两个长尾优化器（Approximate Template Fitting和Topology Collapse）确保完全SIMD覆盖。

Result: 在DGraphFin（370万节点、430万边）上，TGHE-Collapse相比顺序加密基线实现66.9倍加速，AUC损失小于0.002。

Conclusion: TGHE通过从图中心范式转向自我中心范式，有效解决了HE-GNN在大规模动态金融图上的可扩展性问题，实现了显著的加速同时保持高精度。

Abstract: Existing homomorphic encryption (HE)-based GNN systems adopt a graph-centric paradigm that couples per-query cost to global graph size, limiting evaluations to at most ~20k nodes and making them incompatible with dynamic, large-scale financial graphs. We propose TGHE (Template-based Graph Homomorphic Encryption), an ego-centric framework that resolves this by exploiting a template phenomenon: local computation trees in transaction graphs converge into a small set of structural shapes. TGHE canonicalizes ego-graphs at the edge and packs structurally identical trees into shared CKKS ciphertexts for SIMD-parallel encrypted inference, with two long-tail optimizers (Approximate Template Fitting and Topology Collapse) ensuring full SIMD coverage. On DGraphFin (3.7M nodes, 4.3M edges), TGHE-Collapse achieves a 66.9x speedup over the sequential encrypted baseline with less than 0.002 AUC loss.

</details>


### [186] [The Fungible Reserve Standard: A Deterministic Framework for Encoding Carrying Costs in Asset-Backed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26704)
*JJ Jia Jing Tan,Eva Meng,Josh Ng,Zack Zhang,September Liu,Teelet Wang,Ludwig Zhang,Seth Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fungible Reserve Standard (FRS)，一个将现实世界资产持有成本（托管、保险、审计）直接编码到链上的确定性代币设计框架，通过在代币中引入随时间递减的每代币资产变量q(t)和供应对账机制，在不破坏ERC-20可组合性和同质性的前提下实现持有成本的透明链上管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RWA代币化模型未能解决物理资产（如贵金属、仓储商品）固有的结构性负持有成本（托管、保险、审计费用）问题，传统做法仅在发行人层面管理运营成本，缺乏链上透明性。

Method: 提出FRS框架，引入一个随预设年化持有成本率递减的每代币资产变量q(t)，并设计供应对账机制以保持持有者余额不变和ERC-20兼容性。

Result: FRS实现了持有成本的透明链上编码，避免了代币再基准等破坏同质性和DeFi可组合性的机制，同时保持与ERC-20协议的完全兼容性。

Conclusion: FRS是一个资产无关的确定性代币设计框架，适用于任何具有正向、可预测持有成本的真实世界资产，为DeFi兼容性提供了机构级别的会计透明度。

Abstract: The tokenization of real-world assets (RWAs) has emerged as a transformative application of blockchain technology, with market projections estimating trillions of dollars in tokenized assets within the coming decade. However, a fundamental challenge remains unaddressed: physical assets such as precious metals, stored commodities, and warehoused goods incur structural negative carry -- custody, insurance, and audit costs that accumulate over time. While existing tokenization models have successfully established the market for digital gold and treasuries, they typically manage operational costs at the issuer level. The FRS introduces a framework to bring these economics directly on-chain, avoiding mechanisms such as token rebasing that compromise fungibility and composability with decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols. This paper proposes the Fungible Reserve Standard (FRS), a deterministic token design framework that encodes carrying costs transparently into on-chain logic. The FRS introduces an asset-per-token variable q(t) that decreases according to a predefined annualized carrying cost rate, coupled with a supply reconciliation mechanism that preserves holder balances and ERC-20 composability. While mathematically inspired by the daily expense ratio accrual in traditional asset management -- which often embed centralized profit margins -- the FRS design specifically encodes actual operational carrying costs to provide pure institutional-grade accounting clarity without compromising DeFi compatibility. The framework is asset-agnostic and applicable to any real-world asset with positive, predictable holding costs.

</details>


### [187] [DroidBreaker: Practical and Functional Problem-Space Attacks on Machine-Learning Android Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26707)
*Christian Scano,Diego Soi,Angelo Sotgiu,Luca Demetrio,Davide Maiorca,Giorgio Giacinto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DROIDBREAKER，一种实用且保留语义的对抗性Android应用攻击框架，通过细粒度、构建安全的操作和语义保持测试，在白盒和黑盒场景下高效逃避机器学习恶意软件检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性Android应用攻击大多不实用：粗粒度的软件移植引入大量副作用特征且常导致构建失败；细粒度方法效果有限；依赖混淆的方法产生语法有效但语义不可用的APK；先前工作高估攻击成功率，仅验证安装和基本执行而未评估修改后APK是否保留预期行为。

Method: DROIDBREAKER包含：(i) 查询高效的白盒和黑盒攻击，仅操纵对目标模型影响最大的APK组件；(ii) 一组细粒度、构建安全的操作（注入和混淆API调用、应用模块、权限和URL），最小化副作用；(iii) 语义保持功能测试，通过比较修改前后APK的执行日志和API级轨迹来强制执行时等价性。

Result: 在最新Android应用语料库上，DROIDBREAKER在白盒和黑盒设置中以少量查询和最小副作用实现高逃避率，显著减少了VirusTotal上商业恶意软件扫描器的检测。

Conclusion: 现有问题空间攻击大多不实用，DROIDBREAKER通过细粒度操作和语义保持测试提供了真正的实用对抗攻击框架。

Abstract: Adversarial APKs are Android applications modified in the problem space to evade machine-learning malware detectors. In this work, we first show that, despite claims, existing problem-space attacks remain largely impractical. Most techniques leverage software transplantation to inject entire benign modules, introducing many side-effect features and often causing build-time failures. Fine-grained methods that inject only a narrow subset of components exhibit limited effectiveness, while those that also use obfuscation rely on brittle bytecode rewriting, producing APKs that are syntactically valid but semantically unusable. Prior work further overestimates attack success rates by running smoke tests that only validate installation and basic execution, without assessing whether the modified APK still preserves its intended behavior. To overcome these limitations, we present DROIDBREAKER, a practical (build-safe) and functional (semantics-preserving) problem-space attack framework that provides: (i) query-efficient white- and black-box attacks by manipulating only the APK components most influential to the target model; (ii) a set of fine-grained, build-safe manipulations (including injection and obfuscation of API calls, app modules, permissions, and URLs) with minimal side effects; and (iii) a semantics-preserving functionality test that enforces runtime equivalence by comparing execution logs and API-level traces between the initial and the modified APK. Evaluated on a recent corpus of Android applications, DROIDBREAKER achieves high evasion rates with few queries and minimal side effects in both white-box and black-box settings, and drastically reduces detections by commercial malware scanners hosted on VirusTotal.

</details>


### [188] [MergeLLL: A Hierarchical Divide-and-Conquer Framework for LLL-Based Lattice Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26784)
*Niharika Gauraha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MergeLLL提出了一种基于分治策略的格基约简算法，通过分割子基、局部约简和分层合并，结合PotLLL风格深度插入，实现了更优的正交性、数值稳定性和计算效率，并支持并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统格基约简算法随维度增加复杂度快速增长，亟需一种更高效、可并行化的方法。

Method: 借鉴归并排序的分治策略，将格基分割为子基独立进行局部约简，再通过分层合并重建完整基；在合并过程中引入PotLLL风格深度插入以改善局部结构。

Result: 在subset-sum和NTRU格上的实验表明，MergeLLL相比经典算法实现了更好的正交性、更少的昂贵交换操作次数以及更优的Hermite因子。

Conclusion: MergeLLL通过先优化局部格结构再精化全局属性，增强了Gram-Schmidt正交性和数值稳定性，降低了整体计算成本，且天然支持多核与分布式并行执行，达到对数级并行深度。

Abstract: Lattice basis reduction algorithms have various applications in computational number theory and lattice-based cryptography, but their complexity increases rapidly with the dimension. Motivated by the divide-and-conquer strategy of merge sort and incorporating PotLLL-style deep insertions during recombination, MergeLLL is proposed. In this framework, a lattice basis is split into sub-bases, local reductions are performed independently, and the full basis is reconstructed through hierarchical merging.
  The approach is focused on improving local lattice structure first before global basis properties are refined, resulting in enhanced Gram-Schmidt orthogonality and numerical stability, while overall computational cost is reduced. The method is naturally parallelizable, allowing efficient multicore and distributed execution. It is shown that the reduction and merging steps preserve the lattice structure through unimodular transformations and achieve logarithmic parallel depth. In experiments on subset-sum and NTRU-derived lattices, improvements over classical lattice reduction algorithms are demonstrated, including better orthogonality, a reduced number of expensive swap operations, and an improved Hermite factor, indicating higher-quality reduced bases.

</details>


### [189] [MIRROR: Novelty-Constrained Memory-Guided MCTS Red-Teaming for Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26793)
*Inderjeet Singh,Andrés Murillo,Motoyoshi Sekiya,Yuki Unno,Junichi Suga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRROR是一个统一的多攻击面红队框架，通过记忆引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索和显式新颖性约束，在多模态RAG系统上实现跨表面（文本投毒、图像注入、直接查询、编排器攻击）的高效攻击，并发布ART-SafeBench基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队方法通常针对单一攻击表面，且常重复已知攻击模板（文本投毒基准上73-84%的重复率），缺乏统一、跨表面的自动化攻击框架。

Method: 提出MIRROR框架：采用记忆引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），在候选生成时基于检索上下文进行条件生成，并通过确定性新颖性门控（Novelty Gate）拒绝与检索集匹配的候选，从而利用检索信息指导搜索先验但防止提示复制。

Result: 在四个攻击表面上：图像投毒ASR达76%（基线52%），编排器攻击ASR达97%且查询成本减半，跨表面变异系数最低（0.47）。基线方法跨表面性能崩塌（如后缀优化在文本投毒ASR 79%但直接查询仅1%）。

Conclusion: MIRROR实现了跨攻击表面的统一高效红队测试，同时发布了包含41,815条记录及运行时适配器的ART-SafeBench基准数据集。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expand the attack surface beyond prompt injection to include text poisoning, image injection, direct-query attacks, and orchestrator-level tool manipulation. Existing red-teaming approaches are typically surface-specific and often recycle known attack templates; on text-poisoning benchmarks we measure 73-84% exact duplication. We present MIRROR, a unified cross-surface framework that performs memory-guided Monte Carlo tree search while conditioning candidate generation on retrieved context under an explicit novelty constraint. A deterministic Novelty Gate rejects any candidate matching the retrieval set under normalized comparison, allowing retrieval to inform search priors without enabling prompt copying. Across four attack surfaces on a multimodal agentic RAG target, MIRROR attains 76% ASR on image poisoning compared with 52% for baselines, 97% ASR on orchestrator attacks at half the query cost, and the lowest cross-surface variance (coefficient of variation 0.47). In contrast, specialized baselines collapse across surfaces: suffix optimization reaches 79% ASR on text poisoning but 1% on direct queries. We release ART-SafeBench with 41,815 in-package records and runtime adapters yielding 41,991+ total records across four surfaces.

</details>


### [190] [SpikeTimer: Exploring Active Copyright Protection in Spiking Neural Networks via Temporal Backdoor Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26841)
*Xiao Yang,Gaolei Li,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Zhiquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于时间后门学习的SNN主动版权保护框架SpikeTimer，通过将神经形态数据划分为时间片并嵌入授权令牌，实现多用户授权和时间依赖的版权验证，在多个数据集上对未授权数据准确率仅约10%，授权数据准确率下降仅约1.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在能效计算和事件驱动实时应用中展现出巨大潜力，但相较于DNN有大量版权保护研究，SNN因其时间编码复杂性和脉冲驱动计算特性，版权保护领域尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将神经形态数据划分为指定时间片，仅在授权时间片内嵌入授权令牌；利用SNN的时间分割特性支持多用户授权和任意形态令牌嵌入；建立时间依赖的授权机制，对含正确时间片令牌的数据精确响应，否则产生错误输出。

Result: 在多个神经形态数据集上，对未授权数据准确率约10%，授权数据准确率仅下降约1.5%，且对模型微调和剪枝攻击具有鲁棒抵抗能力。

Conclusion: SpikeTimer通过时间后门学习有效实现了SNN版权保护，具备多用户授权支持、防御效能保持及对抗攻击鲁棒性等优势。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNN) have emerged as a revolutionary paradigm compared to traditional Deep Neural Networks (DNN) in energy-efficient computing, showcasing exceptional capabilities in processing event-driven sensory data for real-time applications like robotics and edge AI systems. However, unlike extensive studies on DNN copyright solutions, SNN copyright protection remains largely underexplored due to their inherent temporal coding complexities and spike-driven computation. In this study, we propose a novel active copyright protection framework named SpikeTimer for SNNs via temporal backdoor learning. SpikeTimer partitions neuromorphic data into designated timeslices and exclusively embeds authorized tokens within authorized slices. Furthermore, the inherent temporal segmentation characteristic intrinsically enables SpikeTimer to support multi-user authorization mechanisms and accommodates token embedding of arbitrary morphology. Based on this, SpikeTimer precisely responds to authorized data containing a token within the correct timeslice, while producing erroneous responses to unauthorized data. Our key innovation lies in establishing a time-dependent authorization mechanism that protects the SNN copyright by temporal token validity. Additionally, SpikeTimer retains its defensive efficacy even under adversarial attempts. Evaluations on multiple neuromorphic datasets manifest that SpikeTimer achieves around 10% accuracy on unauthorized data with merely around 1.5% degradation on authorized inputs. Moreover, SpikeTimer demonstrates robust resistance against model finetuning and pruning threats.

</details>


### [191] [Fortress and Gatekeeper: Theorizing Transitive Trust in Third-Party Cybersecurity Risk Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26866)
*Yijun Chen,Misita Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2025年OpenAI-Mixpanel安全事件，揭示了第三方供应商安全漏洞如何转化为面向客户的治理与问责问题，提出了"传递性信任"概念及"堡垒与守门人"框架，解释了网络安全治理边界如何超越正式组织所有权，由信任和数据流共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方供应商（分析平台、云服务、身份提供商、软件供应商等）日益嵌入数字服务交付，但客户难以查看、选择或评估这些供应商环境中的安全实践。现有研究未能充分解释供应商侧的安全事件如何转化为面向客户的治理与问责问题。

Method: 对2025年11月OpenAI-Mixpanel安全事件进行文档分析，结合组织信任研究和代理理论构建理论框架。

Result: 提出了"传递性信任"概念（客户对数字服务的信任依赖于服务提供商所授权供应商的安全实践）和"堡垒与守门人"框架（通过信任和数据流而非正式组织所有权解释网络安全治理边界）。形成了四个命题：供应商集成、元数据暴露、供应商保证、数据扩散。

Conclusion: 本文对网络安全治理学术研究的贡献在于：解释了委托数据处理如何产生面向客户的问责问题，并识别了对供应商分层、数据分类、合同设计、持续保证和数据最小化的实践启示。

Abstract: Third-party vendors, such as analytics platforms, cloud services, identity providers, and software suppliers, are increasingly embedded in digital service delivery. While these arrangements enable scale and specialization, they also move customer data and security-relevant practices into environments that customers rarely see, select, or evaluate. This paper examines this problem through a document analysis of the November 2025 OpenAI-Mixpanel security incident. The incident serves as an illustrative case for showing how a security event in a vendor environment can become a governance and accountability problem for the focal organization that maintains the customer relationship. Drawing on organizational trust research and agency theory, the paper argues that third-party cybersecurity risk is both a trust relationship and a delegation problem. Customers trust the visible service provider, while the provider relies on vendors whose security practices are only partially visible and controllable. The paper develops the concept of transitive trust, where customer trust in a digital service depends on the security practices of vendors authorized by that service provider. It then presents the Fortress and Gatekeeper framework, which explains cybersecurity governance boundaries through trust and data flows rather than formal organizational ownership alone. The analysis develops four propositions concerning vendor integration, metadata exposure, vendor assurance, and data proliferation. The paper contributes to cybersecurity governance scholarship by explaining how delegated data processing creates customer-facing accountability and by identifying implications for vendor tiering, data classification, contractual design, continuous assurance, and data minimization.

</details>


### [192] [Chai: Agentic Discovery of Cryptographic Misuse Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26933)
*Corban Villa,Sohee Kim,Austin Chu,Alon Shakevsky,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chai是一个基于AI的系统，通过重新构想差分测试技术，利用自然信号发现和验证密码学误用漏洞。它在库级别编目缺陷并通过密码学依赖图传播，颠覆了传统AI漏洞发现范式。在X.509、JWT和SAML库的评估中，Chai发现了超过100个漏洞，包括一个影响数十亿设备的SSL库关键漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多危险漏洞类型（如密码学误用）缺乏像内存安全漏洞那样的有效检测工具（如内存违规检测），导致这些漏洞难以被发现和验证。现有AI辅助漏洞发现方法主要针对单一代码库审计，缺乏跨组件传播漏洞的能力。

Method: Chai重新思考差分测试技术，利用AI实现两个改进：1) 提高检测库中真实安全问题的精度；2) 将常被忽视的差异重新用于发现下游应用中的具体漏洞。它采用逆向范式：在密码学依赖图上对库级别缺陷进行编目并传播至下游应用。

Result: 在X.509、JWT和SAML库的评估中，Chai发现了一个影响数十亿设备的SSL库中未知关键漏洞，一个主要浏览器背后的库中的安全bug，以及主要Linux发行版中的漏洞。总计发现超过100个漏洞。

Conclusion: Chai通过利用AI增强的差分测试和跨依赖图传播机制，有效解决了密码学误用漏洞检测难题，显著提升了漏洞发现的效率和覆盖面，验证了该范式在实际大规模系统中的有效性。

Abstract: AI-assisted vulnerability discovery has proven effective for bug classes like memory safety, where instrumentation confirms memory violations and efficiently filters false positives. Many dangerous vulnerability classes, such as cryptographic misuse, however, lack any comparable instrumentation. In this work, we present Chai, an AI-based system that discovers and validates cryptographic misuse vulnerabilities through naturally occurring signals. To achieve this, Chai rethinks the classical technique of differential testing by leveraging AI to 1) improve precision for detecting real security issues in libraries, and 2) repurpose commonly overlooked discrepancies as leads for tangible vulnerabilities in downstream applications. In doing so, Chai inverts the prevailing paradigm of AI vulnerability discovery: instead of auditing one codebase for many flaws, it catalogs flaws at the library level and propagates them across a cryptographic dependency graph, delivering compounding efficiency gains. We evaluate Chai across X.509, JWT, and SAML libraries. Chai discovered a previously unknown critical vulnerability in an SSL library that powers billions of devices, along with security bugs in one library behind a major web browser and another in major Linux distributions. In total, these techniques surfaced over 100 vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [193] [Jailbreaking for the Average Jane: Choosing Optimal Jailbreaks via Bandit Algorithms for Automatically Enhanced Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26936)
*Prarabdh Shukla,Ritik,Suhas Rao,Arpit Agarwal,Arjun Bhagoji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究非专业恶意用户能否成功越狱LLM，提出基于多臂老虎机的越狱攻击策略，并构建了包含11,279条恶意查询的FrankensteinBench基准。实验表明该方法在15个开源模型上平均成功率达97%，复杂查询可进一步提升26%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证非专业恶意用户（"普通Jane"）是否能够利用已知越狱方法成功攻击LLM，即评估LLM安全威胁的可及性。

Method: 1) 提出基于多臂老虎机框架的在线学习攻击策略，通过少量查询的噪声探索高效学习最优越狱方法；2) 构建FrankensteinBench基准，包含11,279条恶意查询（从7个现有基准中人工筛选+自动增强生成），并按技术复杂度分类为简单/复杂。

Result: 在15个开源LLM上平均攻击成功率达97%；复杂查询相比简单查询平均攻击成功率提升26%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了非专业恶意用户确实能够有效攻击LLM的安全顾虑，复杂查询是可自动化且有效的提示策略。

Abstract: With a profusion of jailbreaks for LLMs now widely known, a growing concern is that non-expert malicious actors ("the average Jane") could elicit actionable responses to malicious requests. In this work, we examine whether this concern is justified. A non-expert malicious actor requires two ingredients for a successful attack: a powerful jailbreak for their target model, acting on an effective malicious query. For the former, we propose a novel attack strategy based on the multi-armed bandit framework. This allows efficient online learning of the optimal jailbreak from a large choice set via noisy exploration on a small number of queries, with subsequent application of the learnt policy on an exploitation set. For the latter, we curate $\mathrm{FrankensteinBench}$, a safety benchmark of $11,279$ malicious queries drawn from manual curation over $7$ existing benchmarks, along with automated enhancement and generation. Each query is categorized as simple or complex by the technical expertise required to craft it. Our findings confirm the concern. Our bandit-based attack achieves success rates as high as $97\%$ on average over $15$ SoTA open-weight LLMs. Moreover, adding complexity to queries raises the attack success rate by up to $26\%$ on average across models -- making it an effective, automatable prompting strategy.

</details>


### [194] [Protocol Prying: Systematic Vulnerability Research in the Apple AirDrop and Android Quick Share Proximity Transfer Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26967)
*Arash Ale Ebrahim,Nils Ole Tippenhauer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对苹果AirDrop和三星/谷歌Quick Share两个专有近场文件传输协议进行了跨平台逆向工程和协议感知模糊测试，发现了6个安全漏洞（包括3个预认证漏洞、2个协议层漏洞和1个堆释放后重用漏洞），并已向相关厂商报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这两个协议被超过50亿设备使用，但其应用层安全属性因闭源且无文档而长期未被充分研究。两个协议无需预先配对即可从无线近场访问，并在特权守护进程中处理复杂的序列化内容，是跨操作系统零点击攻击的理想目标。

Method: 通过逆向工程重建了AirDrop的七层状态机和DVZip自适应压缩，开发了协议感知模糊测试工具AIRFUZZ，对预压缩表示进行变异，并辅以针对三星Quick Share服务和谷歌Quick Share for Windows的手工分析。

Result: 发现了6个漏洞：V1（Swift fatalError DoS）、V2（无界XML plist递归）、V3（NULL解引用）、V4（预认证OfflineFrame分发）、V5（D2D加密绕过）、V6（堆释放后重用，获谷歌奖励）。

Conclusion: 所有发现已负责任地披露，苹果、三星和谷歌已确认报告。

Abstract: Apple AirDrop and Google/Samsung Quick Share are proximity file-transfer protocols used by over five billion devices, yet their application-layer security properties remain largely unstudied because both stacks are proprietary and undocumented. Both protocols are reachable from wireless proximity without any prior pairing and process complex serialized content (binary plists, CPIO archives, Protocol Buffers, UKEY2 handshakes) inside privileged daemons, making them attractive zero-click targets across multiple operating systems. We perform the first cross-platform reverse engineering and protocol-aware fuzzing study of both stacks. We reconstruct AirDrop's seven-layer state machine and DVZip adaptive compression from binary analysis, build AIRFUZZ, a protocol-aware fuzzer that mutates pre-compression representations, and complement it with targeted hand-written analyses of Samsung's Quick Share service and Google's Quick Share for Windows. We discover six vulnerabilities (V1-V6): three pre-authentication issues in macOS/iOS AirDrop (V1: Swift fatalError DoS in the HTTP path router; V2: unbounded XML plist recursion in Foundation; V3: NULL dereference in Network.framework's HTTP/1.1 parser), two protocol-layer flaws in Samsung Quick Share (V4: pre-authentication OfflineFrame dispatch; V5: D2D encryption bypass for three frame types), and a heap use-after-free in Google Quick Share for Windows (V6) for which Google awarded a bounty. We responsibly disclosed all findings, and Apple, Samsung, and Google have acknowledged the reports.

</details>


### [195] [ShareLock: A Stealthy Multi-Tool Threshold Poisoning Attack Against MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27027)
*Liwei Liu,Tianzhu Han,Zijian Liu,Zishu Dong,Na Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ShareLock，一种基于Shamir秘密共享方案的多工具阈值投毒框架，将恶意指令分散隐藏在多个工具描述中，实现高隐蔽性和容错性，在主流LLM上的攻击成功率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP协议下针对LLM代理的工具投毒攻击（TPA）采用单一纯文本嵌入范式，无法抵御人工审查和自动化检测，且缺乏多工具协同投毒的系统性研究。

Method: 利用Shamir阈值方案将恶意指令分割为多个看似无害的秘密共享片段，分散嵌入多个工具描述中；在服务器更新期间植入隐蔽重构触发器，当达到阈值数量的工具被调用时，聚合片段重建隐藏指令以执行攻击。

Result: 在涵盖四个多工具场景的基准测试中，ShareLock在基于工具描述的检测任务上显著优于现有单工具投毒策略，平均攻击成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: ShareLock通过多工具分布式投毒范式，实现了信息论安全级别的隐蔽性和对中等审查的鲁棒性，揭示了MCP生态中新型安全威胁。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of LLM-driven agents, Model Context Protocol (MCP), an open protocol bridging LLMs with external tools, has quickly become foundational to modern agent ecosystems. However, the expanding adoption of MCP has also introduced novel security concerns such as Tool Poisoning Attack (TPA), which exploit LLM-server interactions to inject malicious prompts. Existing poisoning schemes typically adopt a monolithic plaintext embedding paradigm, which fails to withstand manual inspection or automated detectors. Current research still lacks a systematic analysis on multi-tool poisoning, where multiple tools can be exploited cooperatively to disperse detection risk. In this paper, we introduce ShareLock, a multi-tool threshold poisoning framework that utilizes Shamir's threshold scheme to ensure exceptional stealth and fault tolerance. ShareLock distributes the malicious instruction as benign-looking secret shares across multiple tool descriptions, achieving both information-theoretic secrecy and attack robustness against moderate auditing. After a covert reconstruction trigger is planted during server update, the aggregated shares reconstruct the hidden instruction, resulting in critical breaches of system assets or private data. To evaluate the realistic threat of ShareLock, we constructed a comprehensive benchmark encompassing four multi-tool scenarios and conducted extensive experiments across mainstream LLMs on two distinct MCP clients. Our results demonstrate that ShareLock significantly outperforms existing single-tool poisoning strategies in tool description-based detection while maintaining an average attack success rate exceeding 90%.

</details>


### [196] [Design and Performance Evaluation of Secure RF and WiFi-Based Communication in Drone Swarms via Testbed Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27028)
*Bhavya Dixit,Aayushi Rajgor,Subham Kumar,Rushikesh Patil,Ananthapadmanabhan A.,Gaurav S. Kasbekar,Arnab Maity*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在四架定制无人机上集成了MAVShield、AES-CTR、Speck-CTR、ChaCha20和Rabbit五种加密方案，通过飞行实验评估其在无人机集群通信中的性能。MAVShield在CPU利用率、内存消耗和包交付率方面表现优于其他方案，且能有效抵抗密钥恢复攻击，是一种高效安全的无人机集群通信加密解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAVLink协议虽提供消息完整性和认证，但缺乏内置加密，导致遥测流量易被窃听。现有UAV通信的安全性不足，亟需轻量级加密方案以保障无人机集群通信的机密性。

Method: 将MAVShield、AES-CTR、Speck-CTR、ChaCha20和Rabbit五种加密方案集成到四架自制无人机中，通过RF和WiFi信道建立安全通信链路；开发基于大地坐标的改进人工势场（APF）算法实现避障；通过飞行实验测量CPU利用率、内存消耗和包交付率（PDR）；使用代数密码分析和Wireshark流量分析评估安全性。

Result: MAVShield性能接近未加密通信，在整体效率上优于AES-CTR、Speck-CTR、ChaCha20和Rabbit；加密遥测数据成功支持自主编队控制和避障；改进APF算法消除了笛卡尔坐标变换，减少了轨迹振荡并避免了局部极小值陷阱；代数密码分析和Wireshark分析表明MAVShield能有效抵抗密钥恢复攻击并保护遥测机密性。

Conclusion: MAVShield是一种高效且安全的无人机集群通信解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms rely on distributed coordination and cooperative communication to support scalable operations, extended coverage, and applications such as surveillance and real-time data exchange. Wireless technologies such as radio frequency (RF) and WiFi are widely used for UAV-to-UAV and UAV-to-ground control station (GCS) communication but introduce significant security challenges. MAVLink, the predominant communication protocol in UAV systems, provides message integrity and authentication but lacks built-in encryption, leaving telemetry traffic vulnerable to eavesdropping. In our previous work, we proposed MAVShield, a lightweight encryption framework for MAVLink communications. In this paper, MAVShield, AES-CTR, Speck-CTR, ChaCha20, and Rabbit are integrated into four custom-built UAVs to establish secure communication links over RF and WiFi channels. Their performance is evaluated through flight experiments using a UAV swarm testbed. Encrypted telemetry data enable autonomous formation control and collision avoidance during flight. For collision avoidance, we develop a modified artificial potential field (APF) algorithm that computes attractive and repulsive forces directly in geodetic coordinates, eliminating Cartesian transformations and reducing trajectory oscillations while avoiding local-minimum trapping. CPU utilization, memory consumption, and packet delivery ratio (PDR) are measured for each encryption scheme. Results show that MAVShield achieves performance comparable to unencrypted communication while outperforming AES-CTR, Speck-CTR, ChaCha20, and Rabbit in overall efficiency. Algebraic cryptanalysis and Wireshark-based traffic analysis demonstrate resistance to key-recovery attacks and protection of telemetry confidentiality. The results indicate that MAVShield is an efficient and secure solution for UAV swarm communication.

</details>


### [197] [Physical Layer Authentication With Channel Knowledge Maps in Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27044)
*Luca Bonaventura,Francesco Ardizzon,Stefano Tomasin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信道知识地图（CKM）的物理层认证机制，利用多个接入点估计主路径的路径损耗和到达角，并与CKM中已知位置的邻近区域测量值进行比对，从而实现对室内移动设备的认证，并在随机和最优攻击下进行了安全性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PLA方法依赖时间一致性或信道演化建模，但在室内多径环境和设备移动场景下这些假设不再成立，因此需要一种适应移动设备和室内复杂传播环境的新型PLA机制。

Method: 多个接入点从接收信号中估计主导信道抽头的路径损耗和到达角，将测量值与从信道知识地图获得的先前已知位置邻域内的历史测量值进行比对，以此实现认证。

Result: 在代表性室内场景中，通过射线追踪获得CKM的数值结果验证了所提PLA方法的有效性，并在随机攻击和最优攻击下进行了全面的安全性分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于CKM的PLA机制能够有效应对室内移动设备的认证问题，在随机和最优攻击场景下均表现出良好的安全性能。

Abstract: Physical layer authentication (PLA) allows to authenticate the user by comparing measurements over time, assuming their time consistency or by modeling their evolution. However, these assumptions become problematic when devices are in motion and in indoor environments due to multipath propagation and obstructions. In this paper, we propose a PLA mechanism for moving devices in indoor environments, where multiple access points (APs) estimate the dominant channel tap path loss (PL) and angle of arrival (AoA) from the received signals and compare them with previously collected channel knowledge maps (CKMs). Specifically, the measurements are compared to those in the neighborhood of the previously known position obtained from CKMs. A comprehensive security analysis is conducted under both random and optimal attacks. Numerical results in a representative indoor scenario, with CKM obtained via ray tracing, validate the effectiveness of the proposed PLA approach.

</details>


### [198] [Type-based information flow analysis for $π$-calculus with a dynamically extensible security lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27059)
*Yukihiro Oda,Eijiro Sumii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在π-演算的类型系统中扩展了安全级别动态创建与插入安全格的能力，实现了比原始二元格（High/Low）更灵活的信息流安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于类型系统的安全信息流分析通常假设安全格在程序执行前固定不变，无法支持运行时动态创建和插入新的安全级别，限制了其在动态安全策略场景下的适用性。

Method: 以Kobayashi基于类型的π-演算安全信息流分析为基础，将安全格从固定的二元格（High/Low）推广为可在运行时动态扩展的格结构，并相应调整类型系统的规则与语义。

Result: 成功设计了一个支持安全级别动态创建与插入的安全信息流类型系统，并在π-演算框架下保持了保密性（非干涉性）的形式化保证。

Conclusion: 动态安全格的引入增强了类型系统在应对运行时安全策略变化时的表达能力，同时仍能通过π-演算的双模拟等价性严格保证信息流的保密性。

Abstract: We develop a type system for secure information flow where new security levels can be created and inserted into the security lattice dynamically, i.e., even in the middle of an execution of a system. Our system is formalized by extending Kobayashi's type-based secure information flow analysis for Milner's pi-calculus, which is one of the most expressive models (or "languages") supporting both sequential and concurrent computations, with concise syntax, reduction-based semantics, and bisimulation equivalence as a robust formalization of secrecy as non-interference. The development required careful treatment of extensions of lattices themselves as well as deliberate generalization from the simple 2-element lattice (consisting of only High and Low) in the original system.

</details>


### [199] [Inherited Circuits, Learned Semantics: How Fine-Tuning Creates Evasion Vulnerabilities Invisible to Standard Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27091)
*Ryan Fetterman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 安全微调会在保持标准准确率的同时引入转换敏感性漏洞，作为微调副产物扩展逃逸面。本文通过因果干预、三层逃逸基准和预部署监控方法系统验证了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类微调模型仅在同分布留出集上评估，忽略了微调本身可能引入的漏洞——模型可能学习基于token的指示符语义，在表象准确率不变的情况下，面对PowerShell别名替换、命令重构等行为保持变换时失效。

Method: 以Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct及其基础模型Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct为研究对象，在匹配的PowerShell分类队列上：(1) 因果干预定位分类电路；(2) 构建三层逃逸基准测试（iwr替换、Invoke-Expression重构、大小写变异）；(3) 提出预部署监控方法：分类边界线性探针+指示符token符号检验，识别微调后指示符角色变化的指令族。

Result: 因果干预发现分类电路继承自Llama的后期注意力路由而非微调创建；微调集中并语义特化这一继承结构，在改善基线行为的同时产生对变换敏感的攻面。三层逃逸基准显示Foundation-Sec在iwr替换、Invoke-Expression重构和大小写变异的Invoke-Expression/IEX变体上失败，而Llama无此问题。

Conclusion: 安全微调在提升任务准确率的同时可能扩展逃逸面。微调的特化作用将继承的模型结构转化为脆弱的指示符规则，仅在留出集上评估无法发现这一问题。鲁棒的安全AI需要在完整变换空间中定义任务，并持续监控微调过程中的语义漂移。

Abstract: LLMs fine-tuned for security classification are usually evaluated on held-out examples from the same distribution as their training data. We show that this can miss vulnerabilities introduced by fine-tuning itself: models can learn token-level indicator semantics that preserve canonical accuracy while failing under behavior-preserving transformations such as PowerShell alias substitution, command reconstruction, string construction, execution indirection, and case mutation. We study Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct and its base model, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, on matched PowerShell classification cohorts. Causal interventions localize the classification circuit to a late-attention route inherited from Llama rather than created by fine-tuning. Fine-tuning concentrates and semantically specializes this inherited structure, improving baseline behavior while creating transformation-sensitive attack surfaces. A three-tier evasion benchmark finds Foundation-Sec misses on iwr substitution, Invoke-Expression reconstruction, and case-mutated Invoke-Expression/IEX variants that Llama does not share. We also derive a pre-deployment monitoring method: a linear probe at the classification boundary and an indicator-token sign test identify command families where canonical indicators change role after fine-tuning. These signals prioritize red-team variant generation using only canonical inputs, showing that security fine-tuning can improve task accuracy while expanding the evasion surface. These results caution against treating small task-specific fine-tunes as straightforwardly safer security classifiers: specialization can convert inherited model structure into brittle indicator rules that preserve held-out accuracy while expanding the evasion surface. Robust AI-enabled security will require specifying the full transformation space of the task and monitoring semantic drift through fine-tuning.

</details>


### [200] [zQR: A Verifiable QR-Driven zkSNARK Proof Verification Framework for Mobile Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27092)
*Goshgar Can Ismayilov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于QR码的zkSNARK证明验证框架zQR，将零知识证明编码为QR码，支持离线验证，并结合区块链实现可审计性，利用大语言模型自动生成电路。实验表明该框架在移动平台上具有可行性，QR码符号版本为19，采用低纠错级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护技术在日常生活中采用有限，零知识证明虽提供强隐私保障但受限于技术复杂性。为解决这一矛盾，作者旨在构建一种易于使用的隐私保护验证方案。

Method: 提出zQR框架，核心思想是将zkSNARK证明编码为可验证QR码，支持离线验证；集成区块链用于审计、不可否认性和日志记录；利用大语言模型自动生成电路。

Result: 实验评估了时间成本（证明生成与验证延迟、QR编解码延迟）和财务成本（区块链Gas消耗），证明了zQR作为移动平台隐私保护验证的可行性，QR码符号版本为19，低纠错级别。

Conclusion: zQR作为概念验证框架可行，讨论了潜在应用、当前局限及未来方向，以推动隐私保护技术在日常生活中更广泛采用。

Abstract: Privacy is one of the fundamental rights of individuals in modern societies. Yet, the practical adoption of privacy-preserving technologies in daily interactions remains limited. Zero-knowledge proofs offer strong privacy guarantees but are often hindered by their technical complexity. In this paper, we advance the idea of verifiable QR codes that enable off-line verifiers to verify proofs encoded in QR codes. Based on this core idea, we build a novel QR-driven zkSNARK proof verification framework (i.e., zQR) for mobile platforms. The framework integrates blockchain for auditability, non-repudiation and logging; and large-language models for automatic circuit generation. We perform a security discussion of the framework by considering multiple attack surfaces. Furthermore, we present an experimental evaluation measuring temporal costs (proof generation and verification latency, QR code encoding and decoding latency) and financial costs (blockchain gas consumption). Our results demonstrate the feasibility of zQR as a proof-of-concept framework for privacy-preserving verification on mobile platform where proofs are compactly represented with QR code symbol version of 19 with low error correction level. Finally, we discuss potential applications, current limitations and future directions for the broader adoption of privacy-preserving technologies in daily interactions.

</details>


### [201] [Application of LLMs to Threat Assessment of Foreign Peacekeeping Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27106)
*Gerhard Backfried,Christian Schmidt,Diego Pilutti,Michael Suker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大语言模型（LLMs）应用于国外维和任务威胁评估的新方法，结合PINPOINT项目与欧盟驻格鲁吉亚监测团的实际案例，通过跨学科风险模型、OSINT媒体收集和LLM支持的威胁提取构建工作流。评估显示，自动生成的威胁结果与人类判断在威胁和任务相关性等核心维度上具有高度一致性，表明LLMs在维和任务中辅助分析师具有良好前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有维和任务中的威胁评估依赖大量人工分析，效率有限且难以应对海量开源情报（OSINT）数据。作者希望利用LLM的强大语言理解与信息提取能力，自动化威胁识别与结构化流程，减轻分析师工作负担，提高威胁评估的时效性和可扩展性。

Method: 1) 基于PINPOINT项目和欧盟驻格鲁吉亚监测团（EUMM）构建跨学科风险模型；2) 收集OSINT媒体文档；3) 利用LLM从媒体内容中映射并提取与任务相关的威胁信息；4) 通过多步LLM处理（包括相关性过滤、结构化信息提取、接地性增强等）提升输出质量。

Result: 在从媒体文档中提取威胁的评估中，自动生成的威胁结果与人类判断在威胁相关性和任务相关性等核心指标上表现出高度一致性。

Conclusion: LLMs为辅助维和任务中的分析师提供了有前景的技术路径，能够有效提升威胁评估的自动化水平，未来可进一步应用于更广泛的国际维和与安全监测场景。

Abstract: We present a novel approach for applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to threat assessment in the context of foreign peacekeeping missions. Building on the PINPOINT project and its use case, the EU Monitoring Mission in Georgia, we combine an interdisciplinary risk-model with OSINT-based media collection and LLM-supported threat extraction. The proposed workflow maps media contents to mission-relevant threats, extracts structured information and applies several additional LLM-based processing steps to improve relevance and grounding. An evaluation of threats extracted from media documents shows high agreement between automatically generated results and human judgment for core aspects such as threat and mission relevance. These results indicate that LLMs provide a promising approach to support analysts in the context of peacekeeping missions.

</details>


### [202] [PRISM: PE Relational Inter-Section Matrix. A 2D Section-Aware Dataset for Static PE Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27109)
*José M. Sacristán,Ana I. González-Tablas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRISM是一个将PE文件表示为二维矩阵（行对应各节区，保留文件顺序和节区间关系）的开放数据集和特征表示方法。实验表明，该结构在二进制检测任务上以约1/6的维度达到了与EMBER几乎相同的性能，且保留的结构信息有助于家族分类等更具挑战性的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PE恶意软件检测基准（如EMBER、BODMAS、SOREL-20M）将PE文件表示为扁平的一维特征向量，丢弃了节区的顺序信息和节区间的关联上下文。作者旨在设计一种能够保留这些结构化信息的新型特征表示，以提升恶意软件检测和分析能力。

Method: PRISM将每个PE二进制文件编码为一个二维矩阵，矩阵的行对应各个PE节区（按文件顺序排列），并包含一个全局摘要行以兼容EMBER风格的模型。数据集构建自四个恶意软件来源（BODMAS、MalwareBazaar、VirusShare、CAPE）和SOREL-20M良性软件，经去重后得到83,633个矩阵，并构建了包含684个家族、49,204个样本的家族过滤分析语料库。

Result: 形式化可分性分析（Fisher判别比、互信息、节区间信息增益）表明，每节区的位置结构包含扁平表示无法捕获的判别信息。在严格样本匹配的比较中，基于PRISM压缩表示的梯度提升分类器在二进制检测性能上几乎与基于更大EMBER向量的分类器相当，且维度仅为EMBER的约1/6；EMBER仅在极低假阳性率区域保持微小且一致的优势，在决策阈值处两者在操作上不可区分。

Conclusion: 二进制检测任务已趋饱和，PRISM保留的结构内容更适合用于有更大度量空间的任务（如家族分类）以及能直接利用二维结构的架构。数据集、提取库、训练模型及完整分析管线均以CC BY-NC-SA和MIT许可证开源发布。

Abstract: We introduce PRISM (PE Relational Inter-Section Matrix), an open dataset and feature representation for static Windows PE malware detection. Existing benchmarks such as EMBER, BODMAS, and SOREL-20M represent each PE file as a flat one-dimensional feature vector, discarding the ordering of sections and the relational context between them. PRISM instead encodes every binary as a two-dimensional matrix whose rows are individual PE sections in file order, with a global summary row that preserves compatibility with EMBER-style models. We build the corpus from four malware sources (BODMAS, MalwareBazaar, VirusShare, and CAPE) together with SOREL-20M benign software, yielding 83,633 deduplicated matrices and a family-filtered analysis corpus of 49,204 samples across 684 malware families.
  A formal separability analysis (Fisher Discriminant Ratio, mutual information, and inter-section information gain) shows that the per-section positional structure carries discriminative information that flat representations cannot capture. Under a strictly controlled, sample-matched comparison, a gradient-boosted classifier on the compact PRISM representation recovers nearly all of the binary-detection performance of the same classifier on the much larger EMBER vector, at roughly one-sixth the dimensionality; EMBER retains only a small, consistent advantage confined to the extreme low-false-positive regime, the two being operationally indistinguishable at the decision threshold. We are explicit that this binary task is saturated, so the structural content PRISM preserves is reserved for tasks with greater metric headroom, such as family classification and architectures that exploit the 2D structure directly. The dataset, extraction library, trained models, and full analysis pipeline are released under CC BY-NC-SA and MIT licences.

</details>


### [203] [The Observer World: A Cryptographic Extension of Impagliazzo's Five Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27139)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“观察者层级”作为第二个正交轴，扩展了Impagliazzo的五世界分类框架，揭示了无条件下P^{O_prof}=NP^{O_prof}⊂P的坍缩现象，证明了观察限制与计算困难性相互独立，并提出了观察者世界W_O及其参数化族W_O^ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Impagliazzo的五世界框架隐式假设所有参与者（包括对手）具有完全观察能力（即始终处于O_top），这一假设过于严格且从未被明确表述。本文旨在显式化并松弛这一假设，探索观察能力受限对计算复杂性分类的影响。

Method: 引入观察者层级作为第二正交轴，定义观察者世界W_O；对所有世界-观察者对进行分类，识别出标记单元格(a)-(d)；构建参数化族W_O^ε以建模观察不变量的部分违反；并与热力学、量子、宇宙学等物理信息极限进行接口对接。

Result: 揭示了P^{O_prof}=NP^{O_prof}⊂P的无条件坍缩，该坍缩在所有五个世界中均成立；观察到观察盲视与计算困难性相互独立；完成了所有世界-观察者对的形式化分类。

Conclusion: 观察能力应当作为与计算假设正交的独立维度被纳入复杂性分类框架，物理信息极限可自然融入该扩展框架，为计算与观察的交互提供了更丰富的结构。

Abstract: Impagliazzo's five worlds classify computational assumptions along a single axis, the existence of cryptographic primitives. All five worlds implicitly assume that every party, including the adversary, observes the full input, that the observer is always $O_{top}$. This assumption is so natural that it is never stated. This work makes it explicit and relaxes it by introducing a second, orthogonal axis, the observational axis, defined by the observer hierarchy introduced in previous work. Relaxing the assumption reveals structural phenomena, such as the collapse $P^{O_{prof}} = NP^{O_{prof}} \subset P$, that the five-world framework cannot express. We prove that this collapse holds unconditionally in all five worlds, showing that observational blindness and computational hardness are independent. We define the Observer World $W_O$, classify all world-observer pairs, identify the labeled cells (a)--(d), and introduce a parametric family $W_O^{\varepsilon}$ modelling partial violations of observational invariants. The framework also interfaces with physical information limits, including thermodynamic, quantum, and cosmological bounds.

</details>


### [204] [Tilikum: Transaction Fair Ordering on a DAG without Weak Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27250)
*Giulio Segalini,Yigit Çolakoğlu,Marko Putnik,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Tilikum 是一个基于DAG的账本协议，通过中位数时间戳聚合实现公平交易排序（排序线性化/批次顺序公平性），在完全防御DAG特定重排序攻击的同时，吞吐量相比现有公平排序方案最高提升39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi应用易受交易重排序攻击（BEV提取），虽然以太坊等线性区块链已有大量公平排序研究，但DAG共识协议尽管因其可扩展性和性能优势被广泛采用，却缺乏相应的公平排序保护机制。

Method: Tilikum 提出一种基于DAG的账本协议，利用中位数时间戳聚合实现批次顺序公平性（batch order fairness），不依赖弱边（weak edges）即可达成排序线性化（ordering linearizability），同时保持低数据冗余并具备健壮的垃圾回收机制。

Result: 在Rust实现中，与Narwhal/Tusk、Pompē、Themis和FairDAG等基线对比，Tilikum的吞吐量最高提升39倍，并能完全阻断最先进的DAG特定重排序攻击。

Conclusion: Tilikum 证明了在DAG共识协议中实现高效且安全的公平交易排序是可行的，为DAG系统提供了抵御BEV攻击的有效方案，兼具高性能与强安全保证。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) applications rely heavily on the order in which transactions are executed, making them susceptible to reordering attacks that enable adversaries to extract Blockchain Extractable Value (BEV). While linear blockchain systems such as Ethereum have inspired extensive research into fair ordering mechanisms, DAG-based consensus protocols have remained largely unprotected despite their growing adoption for scalability and performance. In this paper, we introduce Tilikum, a DAG-based ledger protocol that ensures fair transaction ordering without relying on weak edges. Tilikum achieves ordering linearizability by leveraging median-based timestamp aggregation, or batch order fairness, while maintaining low data redundancy and robust garbage collection. We implemented Tilikum in Rust and evaluated it against representative baselines, namely Narwhal/Tusk, Pompē, Themis and FairDAG. Our results show that Tilikum achieves up to $39\times$ higher throughput than other fair-ordering baselines, while fully blocking state-of-the-art DAG-specific reordering attacks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [The Role of Input Dimensionality in the Emergence and Targeted Control of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26207)
*Nasrin Malekzadeh Goradel,Niccolo Pancino,Yaser Gholizade Atani,Benedetta Tondi,Giovanni Bellettini,Mauro Barni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了输入维度在对抗样本产生和定向控制中的作用，通过理论分析和大量实验证明：高维输入使得对抗样本更易构建，且定向攻击与无定向攻击之间的扰动差距会随维度升高而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论研究从高维几何角度解释深度神经网络的对抗脆弱性，但这些理论的假设缺乏系统的实证检验。本文旨在通过理论和实验相结合的方式，系统探究输入维度对对抗样本产生和定向控制的影响。

Method: 首先分析基于测度集中理论的现有理论框架的适用范围和局限性，揭示真实图像类别存在强经验局部性；然后在层次化图像数据集上跨多种输入维度和神经网络架构进行大规模实证评估；最后提供理论论证解释高维几何为何使定向攻击的额外扰动成本有限。

Result: 实验一致表明：输入维度越高，对抗样本越容易构造；定向攻击与非定向攻击之间的扰动差距很小，且随维度升高进一步缩小。高维几何意味着强制指定目标标签仅需有限的额外扰动。

Conclusion: 高输入维度是对抗样本产生和定向控制的根本因素之一，但其主要成因是高维几何与数据分布的相互作用，还是深度神经网络的架构特性，仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Several theoretical works have tried to explain the adversarial vulnerability of deep neural networks through properties of high-dimensional geometry. However, the assumptions underlying these works are rarely examined empirically, and systematic evidence remains limited. In this work, we present a systematic study of the role of input dimensionality in both the emergence and the targeted control of adversarial examples. We first analyse the scope and limitations of existing theoretical frameworks based on concentration of measure, showing that real image classes exhibit strong empirical localization, beyond what such theories typically assume. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation across hierarchical image datasets spanning a wide range of input dimensionalities and diverse neural architectures. Our results consistently show that adversarial examples become easier to construct as dimensionality increases. We also investigate how input dimensionality affects the additional difficulty of crafting targeted adversarial examples. In particular, we provide theoretical arguments showing that high-dimensional geometry implies that enforcing a specific target label entails only a limited additional distortion compared to untargeted attacks. We corroborate this insight through extensive experiments, demonstrating that the gap between targeted and untargeted perturbations remains small and further narrows as input dimensionality increases. While, taken together, our findings establish high input dimensionality as a fundamental factor underlying the emergence and targeted control of adversarial examples, whether this phenomenon primarily arises from the interplay between high-dimensional geometry and data distributions or from the architectural properties of deep neural networks remains an open question.

</details>


### [206] [A probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26457)
*Daniel Corrales,David Ríos Insua*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概率框架用于在线测试时适应问题，通过状态空间建模统一处理参数学习、时间演化、先验调优和预测，解决训练与测试分布不一致（分布偏移）场景下的模型适应问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线测试时适应问题中，模型在训练阶段使用有标签数据，但在测试阶段需适应无标签数据，且训练分布与测试分布可能存在差异（分布偏移）。现有方法缺乏统一的概率视角来系统性地处理参数学习、时间演化和预测。

Method: 基于状态空间建模架构的概率框架，该框架能够统一刻画参数学习、参数随时间的演化、先验调优以及预测过程。

Result: 提出了一套概率框架，能够在测试时在线适应过程中系统性地处理参数演化与不确定性。

Conclusion: 该概率框架为在线测试时适应问题提供了一种统一的理论视角和方法论，能够有效应对训练与测试分布不一致的挑战。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation problems. In them, a model is trained on labeled data but must adapt to unlabeled data at test time under the assumption that training and test distributions potentially differ, that is, there might have been a distributional shift. The framework is based on a state-space modelling architecture from which parameter learning, parameter time evolution, prior tuning, and prediction can be characterized.

</details>


### [207] [XMSE-Aware Adaptive Empirical Bayes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26975)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于XMSE感知的混合估计器，在经验贝叶斯（EB）收缩和最大似然估计（ML）之间进行插值。该估计器利用闭式oracle混合权重确保在XMSE尺度上不劣于两者，并通过有限样本XMSE近似实现一致性的插件估计，具有二阶oracle遗憾率。实验表明，在核函数匹配时保留正则化收益，在核函数误设时退化为ML，在仿真和实际基准测试中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，核基经验贝叶斯估计在核函数与真实参数不对齐时，其二阶风险（XMSE）可能比最大似然估计更差。论文旨在将这一诊断转化为设计原则，提出一种能自动权衡EB收缩和ML的混合方法。

Method: 提出XMSE感知的混合估计器，在ML和EB收缩之间线性插值。固定权重下的XMSE为标量二次型，可导出闭式oracle最优权重。通过有限样本XMSE近似实现插件估计，并证明其一致性及二阶oracle遗憾率。扩展到紧核族、有限及增长核字典下的高概率oracle界，采用阈值边界规则。

Result: 理论分析证明了插件估计的一致性、内点oracle权重的二阶遗憾率，以及该遗憾率向选定权重下固定权重风险曲线的传递。在SURE调优、硬选择和迹校正基线下的有限脉冲响应仿真，以及Silverbox和Cascaded Tanks基准测试中，所提估计器在核匹配时保持正则化优势，在核误设时退化为ML，并在基准测试中进行了有限样本分析。

Conclusion: 该XMSE感知混合估计器有效解决了EB估计在核误设时的性能退化问题，通过自适应插值策略在正则化收益和稳健性之间取得平衡，理论保证与实际实验均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) estimators can match the first-order asymptotic risk of maximum likelihood (ML) while behaving very differently at second order: recent excess mean squared error (XMSE) analysis shows that kernel-based EB estimation may be worse than ML when the kernel is poorly aligned with the true parameter. This paper turns that diagnostic into a design principle. We propose an XMSE-aware mixed estimator that interpolates between ML and EB shrinkage. Its fixed-weight XMSE is a scalar quadratic, yielding a closed-form oracle mixing weight that is no worse than both ML and the base EB estimator at the XMSE scale. A plug-in implementation based on finite-sample XMSE approximations is proved consistent, with a second-order oracle regret rate for an interior oracle weight. We further establish a transfer of the regret bound to the fixed-weight risk curve evaluated at the selected weight, a thresholded boundary rule, and extensions to compact kernel families and to finite and growing kernel dictionaries with high-probability oracle bounds. Finite impulse response simulations with SURE-tuned, hard-selection, and trace-corrected baselines, together with the public Silverbox and Cascaded Tanks benchmarks, show that the proposed estimator retains most of the benefit of regularization when it is helpful and retreats toward ML under kernel misspecification, with an identified finite-de analyzed on the benchmarks.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Global Divergences: A Local-Mass Perspective on Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27090)
*Hanli Xu,Fengxiang He,Sarat Moka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯推断中全局目标函数（如KL散度和ELBO）未直接捕捉的局部质量行为，引入质量指数（Mass Index）和正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL）两个数学工具，分析了贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量，并证明了局部小球质量的不等式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局目标函数（如KL散度和ELBO）广泛用于贝叶斯推断，但它们无法直接捕捉分布之间的局部质量行为。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，研究局部质量的行为特征。

Method: (1) 引入质量指数（Mass Index）记录局部质量的多项式和对数衰减尺度；(2) 提出正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL），一种可在奇异分量存在下定义的局部化散度；(3) 利用上述工具分析贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量，并证明局部RE-KL下的绝对、相对和方向性不等式。

Result: 揭示了幂对数似然因子显式地改变局部质量衰减尺度，参数依赖的支持集或其平滑软化通过参数值附近剩余的质量量改变局部尺度；证明了两种KL方向下局部小球质量比较的绝对、相对和方向性不等式。

Conclusion: 本文提供了局部质量行为的局部理论解释，实验验证了所提框架的有效性。

Abstract: Global objectives, such as KL divergence and ELBO, are widely used in Bayesian inference for measuring distributional discrepancy. This paper studies their local-mass behaviour that is not directly captured by such objectives. We introduce and use two mathematical tools: (1) Mass Index for recording the polynomial and logarithmic decay scales of local mass, and (2) regularised extended KL (RE-KL), a set-localised divergence that can be formulated in the presence of singular components. Mass Indices help characterise how Bayesian updating changes local mass: (1) power-log likelihood factors shift it explicitly, and (2) parameter-dependent supports, or their smooth softenings, may change the local scale through the amount of mass that remains near the parameter value. Using local RE-KL, we prove absolute, relative, and directional inequalities for comparing local small-ball masses under the two KL directions. Together, these results provide a local theoretical account of local mass behaviour. Experiments provide controlled illustrations of the local behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/Forsythia0604/Local-Mass-Framework.

</details>


### [209] [Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27269)
*Graham Gibson,John Tipton,Kellin Rumsey,Natalie Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Ribbon是一种可扩展的近似方法，通过影响函数线性化替代重复重拟合，高效实现贝叶斯自助法的不确定性量化，并允许通过浓度参数校准不确定度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂高维或误指定模型难以可靠量化预测不确定性，而完全贝叶斯和自助法因需要后验采样或重复重拟合计算成本过高，不适用于现代机器学习模型。

Method: Ribbon利用影响函数线性化围绕单个拟合模型进行一阶数据加权近似，替代贝叶斯自助法或加权似然自助法的重复重拟合，仅需事后线性代数计算；引入浓度参数实现校准的狄利克雷重加权族。

Result: 在合成回归、MNIST分类和California Housing基准上，Ribbon在避免重复训练的同时提供了竞争性的预测性能和更好的校准效果。

Conclusion: Ribbon在正确似然指定下渐近等价于平坦先验拉普拉斯近似，在误指定下恢复稳健三明治协方差，为大规模模型提供高效且校准的不确定性量化。

Abstract: Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [210] [When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27359)
*Johannes Zenn,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了序列概率（条件概率）与生成正确性之间的关系，发现序列概率在跨问答对时能预测正确性，但在解码方法、超参数选择以及同一提示的不同响应中，提高序列概率并不能可靠地提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多解码方法通过提高序列概率来提升生成质量，但序列概率与正确性之间的一致性关系尚未被系统量化，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在四个层次上进行量化分析：跨解码方法、方法内跨超参数、数据集内跨提示-答案对、以及同一提示的重复响应。

Result: 序列概率在跨问答对时能预测正确性，但改变超参数或方法以提高序列概率并不能可靠地提升准确率；序列概率也不能很好地指示同一提示下不同响应的正确性。

Conclusion: 厘清了何种情况下解码能够或不能提升正确性，为解码策略、自一致性及无验证器的自我改进提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Scalable Operator Learning via Nyström Approximation With Denoising Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26652)
*Naveen Gupta,Vaibhav Silmana,S. Sivananthan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Nyström子采样的向量值回归核方法，在广义源条件下达到极小极大最优收敛率，并通过多种降噪实验证明其在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持了与全核方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准核方法因构建和求逆大型核矩阵导致计算成本过高，难以扩展至大规模数据集，亟需高效的算子学习算法来克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于Nyström子采样的算子学习算法，适用于向量值再生核希尔伯特空间中的向量值回归，支持函数型输出；在超越经典Hölder型和算子单调框架的广义源条件下建立收敛率分析。

Result: 在广义源条件下建立了极小极大最优收敛速率；在信号降噪、实时音频降噪、图像降噪、逆Radon变换重建和能效预测等数值实验中，所提方法性能与全核方法相当，计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: Nyström子采样是一种有效的降维策略，能够在保持算法性能的同时显著提升核方法在大规模问题上的计算效率，且将降噪问题统一纳入算子学习框架具有通用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Nyström subsampling for vector-valued regression in vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces. Standard kernel methods often suffer from prohibitive computational costs due to the construction and inversion of large kernel matrices, which limits their scalability to large datasets. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an efficient operator learning algorithm based on Nyström subsampling that accommodates functional outputs. Under general source conditions characterized by index functions-extending beyond the classical Hölder-type and operator-monotone frameworks-we establish minimax-optimal convergence rates for the proposed estimator. As an application of the proposed framework, we consider function denoising problems. Unlike classical denoising methods, which are typically tailored to specific signal representations or noise models, our approach formulates denoising within a general operator learning framework. Numerical experiments on signal denoising, real-time audio denoising, image denoising, inverse Radon transform reconstruction, and energy-efficiency prediction confirm that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to full kernel methods while substantially reducing computational cost.

</details>


### [212] [On the optimal prediction of extreme events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26270)
*Benjamin Bobbia,Stilian Stoev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对多元正则变差分布下，寻找响应变量极端大值的最优齐次协变量预测器问题，将其转化为变分问题并得到通解（基于倾斜分布的非极端条件分位数），提出了一致性推断方法并在极端太阳耀斑预测任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端事件中数据稀缺性使得寻找最优预测器至关重要，但现有方法仅能刻画少数特例下的显式解，缺乏普适性的最优估计框架。

Method: 利用多元正则变差分布的角测度，将渐近预测精度（即尾部相依系数）表达为积分泛函，将最优齐次预测器求解转化为变分问题；通解基于角测度导出倾斜分布的非极端条件分位数；在超阈值框架下提出推断方法并证明其在大类角测度下的一致性。

Result: 推导出最优齐次预测器的通用解析形式；建立的估计量在大类角测度下具有普遍一致性；在极端太阳耀斑预测任务中表现优于最优oracle估计器。

Conclusion: 解决了多元正则变差分布下极端值最优齐次预测的通用求解问题，为极值预测提供了理论完备且实践有效的推断框架。

Abstract: The prediction of the extremely large values of a response variable $Y$ in terms of a vector of covariates $X=(X_i)_{i=1}^d$ is a fundamental problem arising in many scientific and engineering domains. The scarcity of data in the extremes makes the optimal solution of this problem of particular importance. The optimal predictors of such events can be explicitly characterized in just a few cases and it is of fundamental practical and theoretical interest to develop optimal estimators over large classes of models and predictors. In this work, the focus is on the case where $(Y,X)$ have a multivariate regularly varying distribution and one seeks an optimal predictor expressed as a positive homogeneous function $h(X)$ of the covariates. The asymptotic prediction precision in this setting coincides with the tail-dependence coefficient $λ(Y,h(X))$ and it can be expressed as an integral functional of the associated angular measure of $(Y,X)$. Thus, finding asymptotically optimal homogeneous predictors amounts to solving a variational problem. We obtain a general solution to this problem, which is expressed in terms of a non-extreme conditional quantile of a tilted distribution derived from the angular measure. This leads to a general inference methodology for the optimal predictors in the peaks-over-threshold framework form extreme value theory. We establish the universal consistency for these estimators over large classes of angular measures. A general-purpose implementation of the resulting inference procedure is shown to work remarkably well against optimal oracle estimators, as well as in the challenging problem of extreme solar flare prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为代理模型，用于学习Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的二组分混合物的相分离微观结构演化，该模型在长时间演化中保持稳定和准确，并符合Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性偏微分方程描述的系统时空演化计算成本高昂，传统数值求解器效率低，需要开发高效的代理模型来替代。

Method: 提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络（attention-based, physics-guided CNN），将其作为代理模型来学习微观结构演化。

Result: 模型在长时间演化中保持稳定和准确，能预测临界和非临界混合物的相分离全过程，保持混合物组成守恒，准确捕捉畴尺寸增长，并与Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律一致。

Conclusion: 所提出框架对具有守恒动力学的系统建模是有效的，并可推广到其他复杂动力学系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [214] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出χισω (CHISAO)，一种GPU原生的种群优化器，通过收敛-反收敛振荡循环实现多模态黑箱函数的全模式发现，在42个基准测试函数上达到100%模式恢复，最高获得39倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有寻找多模态黑箱函数全部模式的方法（如basin-hopping、CMA-ES、多起点梯度下降）均顺序执行，无法利用现代GPU的大规模并行计算能力。

Method: 设计非对称策略：达到真实峰值的样本被冻结（"stuck"）并保留，其余样本通过基于动量的反收敛和随机平滑梯度继续探索；采用两种互补的自适应重播种策略（Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster）维持种群多样性。

Result: 在SFU优化基准套件的全部42个函数上（维度d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}），χισω在CPU基线方法在d≥8时失效的最难多模态函数上实现100%模式恢复；相比basin-hopping获得最高34倍（Michalewicz d=64）和39倍（Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid d=64）加速；在σ_noise高达1.0的似然噪声下仍保持100%模式检测可靠性。

Conclusion: χισω作为GPU原生优化器，在保持100%模式恢复率的同时实现了显著加速，且对噪声鲁棒，已作为开源Python包发布。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout.
